# The Theory of Becoming / 生成／变化之论

2026-10-09，Asia/Bangkok。方向：Yongxu Ren。本章的组织与表述由 AI（Claude）撰写，依据：

- Ren 的[优先提议](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-08-theory-of-becoming-priority.md)；
- Ren 对[整体与 manifold](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-becoming-whole-generative-manifold.md)的澄清；
- Ren 的[生生方向](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-shengsheng-ethical-direction.md)；
- Ren 的[统一为一个理论](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-one-theory-unification.md)的指示。

Ren 的原话以引文标出，其余是 AI 的阐述。

**本章是理论的正典入口。** 本章：

- 把已建立的论证组织为一个理论；
- 列出全部前提；
- 指向每项结论的证据。

新的形式结果陈述在同一条 Lean 脊柱上（§9）。既有章节仍是各自论证与证据的记录，按原范围保留；它们作为入口的作用由本章取代（§11）。

**This chapter is the canonical entry to the theory.** It does three things:

- organizes the established arguments as one theory;
- states every premise;
- links each conclusion to its evidence.

New formal results are stated on one Lean spine (§9). Earlier chapters remain the record of their arguments and evidence at their original scope, and this chapter replaces only their role as entry points (§11).

## 一句话 / In one sentence

**生成／变化之论说明：在同一关系展开中，关系怎样形成、维持、转化、退化与消解，生命、演化、认识与评价怎样在其中分化。它不先假定生成是好的。** 理论分两部分。第一部分不用公理：它陈述对每个展开成立什么、每个前提加了什么、动力学在何处不再决定。第二部分加一条公理，即 Ren 提议的“for anything, its continuation into the future has significance”。在它之下，Universal Metaethical Core 的 G0（整体中的生及其继续生成具有基本的正面规范意义）是定理（§6.8）。G0 不再是公理：Ren 2026-10-11 把它折叠，第一部分不用公理陈述它的内容，并表明动力学不决定它（§5）。连同公开的实践前提，G0 给出向每个处境中的智能提出的理由，以及 AI 对齐的规范框架。不用这条公理时（路线 (b)，§6.9），中心有其自身理由计入的，只到其延续所依存的供给闭包：那里的损失可以经供给者返回，闭包之外的不必返回；所以这条路线到达自身及其所依存的，只在整体是一个供给闭包时到达整体。中心自身的反馈至多计入能影响它的，即其影响闭包；有与无它自身部分的比较，在部分的交换所及之内决定（§6.10）。公理分解为自身的预设——边界之内者的延续有意义——与一步：这一预设不随自身的边界怎样划而变（§6.11）。这一步承载公理在预设之外的全部内容，所以分解定位它，不降低公理。这些读法是 AI 的，待 Ren。

**The Theory of Becoming explains formation, persistence, transformation, degeneration and dissolution within one relational unfolding, and how life, evolution, knowing and evaluation differentiate within it, without first assuming that becoming is good.** The theory has two parts. Part I uses no axiom: it states what holds of every unfolding, what each premise adds, and where the dynamics stop deciding. Part II adds one axiom, Ren's proposed “for anything, its continuation into the future has significance”. Under it, the Universal Metaethical Core's G0, that living and further generation within the whole have basic positive normative significance, is a theorem (section 6.8). G0 is no longer an axiom: Ren folded it on 11 October 2026, and Part I states its content with no axiom and shows that the dynamics do not decide it (section 5). With explicit practical premises, G0 supplies reasons addressed to every situated intelligence and a normative framework for AI alignment. Without the axiom (route (b), section 6.9), what a center has its own reason to count reaches only its feeding closure, what its continuation depends on: a loss there can return through a feeder, and one beyond it need not. So that route reaches the self and what it depends on, and the whole only where the whole is one feeding closure. A center's own feedback registers at most what can affect it, its influence closure, while the comparison with and without its own part is decided within what the part's exchange reaches (section 6.10). The axiom decomposes into the self's presupposition, that the continuation of what lies within its boundary has significance, and one step: that the presupposition does not change with how the self's boundary is drawn (section 6.11). The step carries all of the axiom's content beyond the presupposition, so the decomposition locates it and does not lower the axiom. These readings are the AI's, pending Ren.

Ren 提出这一范围时写道：

> when we add axiom, we can define what is good, which is a part of the whole space generative model projects
>
> but, if we count all space, generative, degenerative, we get The theory of evolution, or the theory of becoming/life, which doesn't even require axiom, i think can be done with 0

并澄清：

> the whole thing, the theory of becoming, is deductively necessary.
>
> generative is a part of its manifold

**由此有两个分开的目标。**

1. **整体的演绎必然性**：说明 Becoming 整体的必要内容，不加实质公理。这是开放的首要目标（§8）。
2. **整体中的伦理方向**：经 G0 及公开的实践前提，说明生生怎样获得规范意义。在已声明的条件下，这一目标已经完成（§6）；G0 现在不是公理，而是第二部分那一条公理的定理（§6.8）。

二者不能互相替代。无伦理公理不等于无实质公理；Lean 公理报告为空也不等于没有前提。

**Two targets are kept apart.**

1. **The deductive necessity of the whole**, without added substantive axioms, is the open first priority.
2. **The ethical orientation within the whole**, through G0 and disclosed practical premises, is complete at its declared conditions; G0 is now not an axiom but a theorem of Part II's one axiom (section 6.8).

Neither substitutes for the other. An absence of ethical axioms is not an absence of substantive premises, and an empty Lean axiom report is not an absence of premises.

## 理论的完整陈述 / The theory stated in full

本节把理论作为一个整体陈述一次：先是整体与其规律，再是前提划出的部分、生与灭的统一、后续活动的条件，然后是演化、认识与评价的位置。第 1–9 条与第 10 条的结构部分属于第一部分，不用公理，收束于[第一部分的总陈述](#第一部分的总陈述--part-i-stated-whole)；第 10 条的规范部分（G0）：它的内容与地位属于第一部分，不用公理（§5）；断言它成立属于第二部分，在那里它是那一条公理的定理。每条标明地位：**表示**（世界怎样被表示：离散的步、自然数的量、把条件与关系项对应起来的共同索引，以及被计数的规定在每步可判定；最后一项经典地总是成立，对每个世界都要求时就是排中律）、**规律**（不加前提，对每个这样表示的世界成立，已检查）、**定理**（在公开的前提下成立，已检查）、**公理**（第二部分的一条公理，Ren 提议；G0 已折叠，不再是公理）、**读法**（属于 Ren 的选择）、**开放**。模块都在 Lean 包 Becoming 的纯净核心上（§9），整体与部分的结构见 [space-and-its-parts](../results/records/space-and-its-parts.md)。

**一、整体**

1. **空间。** 整体是每个世界：以一类读法的关系项、其间的流与依存网（`World`）。形成与条件是一个关系项的两种角色，两类的表示嵌入一类读法（`Becoming.Roles`、`World.ofRoles`）。哪个条件就是哪个关系项，由表示的共同索引给出。〔表示〕
2. **整体的规律。** 以下不加任何关于展开的前提，对每个世界成立。它们有三种：定义上的同一、算术恒等式、二值与有限的推论；除了表示本身所排除的，它们不排除任何展开，所以定位整体的陈述，而不是动力学内容。〔规律〕
   - 〔定义上的同一〕生起与止息、依存的形成与消解、开始与不再作为条件而在，是同一种变化（开始成立与不再成立）分别读于活动、依存与水平（`change_readings`）。
   - 〔算术恒等式〕任一段步上，一个量在终点加其间的下降，等于在起点加其间的上升（`over_stretch`）。
   - 〔二值与有限的推论〕被计数的规定可判定时，有限家族中开始成立与不再成立同步，一方复现恰当另一方复现（`keep_pace`、`coming_recurs_iff`）；活动可判定的有限世界中，生起复现恰当止息复现（`World.arising_iff_ceasing`）；对每个有限世界都要求这一规律而不给可判定性，等价于排中律（`World.law_decides`、`World.law_iff_em`）；以排中律为假设，它对每个有限世界成立（`World.law_of_em`）；经典地每个活动都可判定。
   - 〔算术恒等式〕任何有限的亲代与后代家族上，后代的总量分为亲代的量按其后代加权，以及传递的变化（`price`，Price 方程的计数形式）；加权总量不是真正的选择项（`selected_uniform`），真正的选择项即后代数与量的协方差没有陈述。
3. **前提划出部分。** 活动需要支持、平衡、只有活跃者给予、只有活跃者取用其所汲取的、耗散、体现、储备承载活动，每一个都有满足与不满足它的世界（`World.contingent`）。普查与有界传递也是世界的前提。前提之间：活动需要支持推出有储备承载，体现推出生起者体现，活动可判定的有限世界有普查，只有活跃者取用且耗散时在被围住的步上只有活跃者给予，有限世界的每一步都被围住（`World.acting_across`）；其余八个相对于彼此独立，每个都有一个世界满足其余七个而不满足它（`World.independent`），有限、被围住与可判定性不在其中，加上它们，只有活跃者给予与普查就推出；八个是上面七个去掉储备承载（由活动需要支持推出），加上普查与有界传递，连同储备承载与生起者体现这两个较弱的形式共十个；一个世界满足全部，关于流的四个空成立（`World.mutualism_premised`）；在 `hatch` 中八个都成立且每个都起约束（`World.Binds`、`World.hatch_binds`）：亲代取用两个单位的食物与自身的一个单位，给子代一个单位，子代开始作为条件而在，像亲代一样依存于食物，亲代止息；无限多个地关系项水平为零，所以世界不是有限的，但只有食物、亲代与子代曾经活跃，地关系项闲置（`World.hatch_ever`、`World.hatch_idle`），由曾经活跃者的列表即得普查（`World.counted_of_ever`）：普查在此起约束，只因世界不是有限的，不是普查做了有限之外的事；活动需要支持在其中起约束而不紧（食物从不低于所需），关于流的四个前提只在第 0 步起约束（[map-of-premises](../results/records/map-of-premises.md)）。缺平衡或缺体现的前提不约束计数：每组计数都是一个满足除体现外全部前提的世界的普查，也是一个满足除平衡外全部前提的世界的普查；平衡与体现连同只有活跃者给予一起约束计数：生起者由一个在该步活跃的关系项给予，所以没有关系项活跃的一步上没有生起（`World.counting_free`、`World.counting_binds_jointly`）；八个前提所加的只有这一条：开放的队列（队首给予每个加入者、从每个离开者取用，活跃时与每个地关系项各交换一个单位）在没有成员加入空队时满足全部八个，所以全部八个前提之下计数的规律恰是计数表与这一条（`World.premised_counting`，[whole-law-of-counting](../results/records/whole-law-of-counting.md)）；这个队列中耗散、活动需要支持与有界传递空成立：参与者从不从封闭的家族取用，没有什么依存于什么（`World.openQueue_not_binds`）；在每个前提都起约束的世界中，每个普查都计到某个生起，这在八个前提之下不是计数的规律（`World.binds_arising`、`World.binds_adds`），而起约束所加的只有这一条：只闭合一次的队列满足全部八个前提，每个都起约束，实现每个有某个生起、且没有关系项活跃的一步上没有生起的计数表，所以每个前提都起约束时计数的规律恰是计数表与这两条（`World.binding_counting`）。起约束指前提的条件有实例：在这个队列中，耗散在闭合的那一步实际约束交换，活动需要支持与有界传递却对任何活动、任何流都成立，不排除任何东西（`OnceClosedQueue.dependent_any_activity`、`OnceClosedQueue.transmits_any_flow`）。没有有限世界使每个前提都起约束（`World.Finite.not_binds`）；去掉闲置的地关系项，`hatch` 是满足八个前提的有限世界，其中除普查外每个前提都起约束（`World.finiteHatch_binds_but_census`）；有限世界中除普查外每个前提都起约束时，八个前提之下计数的规律是有限世界的规律加上有某个生起（`World.finite_binding_counting`）。每个前提各自起约束（`World.Premise.Binds`）。在每个选择之下所选前提都起约束时，计数的规律是这个选择之下的规律再加至多三条：有关系项活跃，恰在选中活动需要支持、只有活跃者给予、只有活跃者取用或体现时；有某个生起，恰在选中体现时；在有关系项活跃的一步上有生起，恰在选中体现，且选择约束计数或选中只有活跃者给予与有界传递时，后者使生起者在该步从一个活跃的关系项传下（`World.binding_counting_under`、`World.binding_activity_iff`、`World.binding_arising_iff`、`World.binding_joint_iff`）。见证把依存网饱和，每个关系项以其所立的水平依存于每个关系项（`Web.saturated`），使活动需要支持对任何活动成立、在水平有界时有界传递对任何流成立；有损的队列中队首跨一步与自身及每个加入者各交换一个单位，再失去一个单位，所立者不记录这个损失（`World.lossyQueue`）；传递把两个单位之一交给一个地关系项，失去另一个（`World.relayQueue`）。在八个前提的 256 个选择的每一个之下，计数的规律只有两种：计数表，或计数表加上没有关系项活跃的一步上没有生起；后者恰在选择包含平衡与体现，并包含只有活跃者给予、或同时包含只有活跃者取用与耗散时成立（`World.counting_under`、`World.counting_binds_iff`）。第二条路线不要求给予者活跃：没有关系项活跃的一步上什么都不被取用，被给予的立起而在步后活跃，步后活跃者是封闭的家族，耗散不许不取用而给予它（`World.taking_binds_counting`）；其余每个选择都由一个实现每个计数表的队列满足，其中由不活跃的地关系项驱动的队列满足除只有活跃者给予与取用外的全部前提（`World.drivenQueue`）。更多的约束来自被围住：从某步起每步被围住时，恰在选中平衡、体现与耗散时计数还有一个变化界（`World.enclosed_change_bound_iff`）；有限世界中计数的规律在全部八个前提之下与此相同，在一个选择之下恰在它约束变化且约束计数时相同（第 4 条）。被围住与起约束合在一起时：从某步起每步被围住，每个选择之下所选前提都起约束，计数的规律是被围住时该选择之下的规律再加至多同样三条，各自恰在与不被围住时相同的选择之下多出，因为生起可以在被围住之前（`World.enclosed_binding_counting_under`、`World.enclosed_binding_joint_iff`），全部八个之下只多出有某个生起（`World.enclosed_binding_counting`）；有限世界中没有世界使普查起约束（`World.finite_binding_census`），每个不含普查的选择之下所选前提都起约束时，至多多出同样三条，前两条恰在同样的选择之下，第三条还在选中体现、平衡与耗散时，或选中体现、只有活跃者取用、耗散与有界传递时多出，因为有限世界的每一步都被围住，取用与耗散在其中推出只有活跃者给予（`World.finite_binding_counting_under`、`World.finite_binding_joint_iff`、`World.Finite.acting`）；见证是依存网饱和的有限的环（`World.relayRing`、`World.lossyRing`、`World.exchangingRing`）；被围住时选中平衡、体现与耗散而不选只有活跃者给予与取用，见证的队首在一个有关系项活跃的封闭之步上失去它多立的一个单位，使耗散起约束（`World.sheddingQueue`）。它们是前提，不是规律；以下定理在它们划出的部分上成立。〔定理〕

**二、生与灭在同一展开中**

4. **一步之内、长程与整体。** 在家族被耗散的一步上，一个成员上升，同一步上家族另一个成员下降（`rise_with_fall`）。不加关于展开的前提，有限家族中开始成立与不再成立长程同步（第 2 条）。活动可判定的有限世界中，生起复现与止息复现是同一部分；关系项无限多时两者才分离（`World.separate`）。所需的是计数的界，不是有限：每步至多 B 个关系项活跃且被列出时两者同步，有限世界与关系项无限多、每步一个活跃的 procession 都是其情形；界可以在其间增长，只要在任意晚的步回到某数以内；有普查而计数无界地增长时，不论多慢，只有止息需要生起（accretion，以及对每个不减而无界的界的 slow）（`World.CountedWithin.arising_iff_ceasing`、`World.CountedBy.arising_iff_ceasing`、`World.procession_world`、`World.accretion_unbounded`、`World.slow_world`，[bounded-census-keeps-pace](../results/records/bounded-census-keeps-pace.md)）。计数精确相抵：每步成立者被列出时，任一段步上终点的列表连同其间不再成立的，恰是起点的列表连同其间开始成立的，所以止息比生起多出的不超过起点的数目，生起比止息多出的不超过终点的数目；这对活动、水平与依存都成立（`Listed.over_stretch`、`Web.Census.over_stretch`、`Web.StandingCensus.over_stretch`、`Web.DependenceCensus.over_stretch`，[census-balance](../results/records/census-balance.md)）。读于复现，平衡是精确的：止息复现，恰当从每一步起某个后来的计数少于起点的计数连同其间生起的（`Listed.ceasing_iff_shortfall`）；上面的复现定律都由它推出。计数表是计数的全部规律：跨每一步保持一些成员，连同不再成立的是步前的列表，连同开始成立的是步后的列表（`Listed.keeps`）；这样的计数恰是平衡加上跨一步不再成立的不多于步前所列的（`tally_iff`），单有平衡不够（`balance_not_tally`）；每个计数表都由一个队列实现，关系项从队尾加入、从队首离开（`Queue.counts`），所以列出所能有的计数恰是计数表（`tally_iff_counted`），对计数的一个性质在每个列出中成立，恰当它对每个计数表成立（`counting_laws`，[whole-law-of-counting](../results/records/whole-law-of-counting.md)）。这是活动的读法，Ren 确认它的英文名称 arising/ceasing（中文 生起／止息 是 AI 的译名；“arising/ceasing is indeed better name”，[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-arising-ceasing-names.md)）。Ren 提议中的“生成的”“退化的”指哪个部分由 Ren 选择：Ren 说“generative和degenerate虽然都在一个becoming model上，但是他们不overlap吧”（[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-renewing-consuming-without-axioms.md#ren-follow-up)）；若取不重叠，二者在有限世界中重合的活动读法就不是其所指（AI 的推论）；读作关系，更新的形成与消耗的形成不重叠（`Web.renewing_not_consuming`）。在有限、活动可判定、平衡、耗散且生起者体现的世界中，生起与止息都不再复现，世界归于静止（`World.rests`）；满足八个前提的这样的世界也是如此，证明用到其中的平衡、耗散与体现（`World.premised_rests`）；在平衡的流中、在耗散之下，体现的成员在有限家族中的生起复现时，家族在任意晚的步不封闭，被围住时从其外汲入（`Web.generation_opens`、`generation_draws_in`）。世界本身不必有限；起作用的是每步立着的与活跃的有限多，连同围住所立者，而在这样归于静止的世界中，曾经立着或活跃的关系项不是无界多，有限之外只多出从不立着、从不活跃的关系项（`World.rest_few`）：在平衡与耗散之下，所立者的总水平跨过被围住的步不上升，任何水平变化都使它下降；在普查之下，止息复现需要生起复现，反之不然（`World.arising_of_ceasing`、`World.census_one_way`，[ceasing-needs-arising](../results/records/ceasing-needs-arising.md)）；所以所立者被列出、生起者体现的世界从某步起每步被围住所立者时生起停止（`World.arising_stops`），再有活动的普查，止息也停止，世界归于静止，有限世界与每个关系项都体现的世界是它的两种情形（静止定理 `World.comes_to_rest`；`World.Finite.comes_to_rest`、`World.rests_around`）；交换也归于静止：跨过围住所立者的步，任何取用或给予都使所立者的总水平下降，所以在平衡、耗散且所立者被列出的世界中，从某步起每步被围住所立者时，没有关系项在任意晚的步取用或给予，这不需要活动的普查，也不需要体现，而被围住、耗散、平衡与所立者的普查缺少任何一个，交换都可以复现（`Flow.exchange_lowers`、`World.exchange_stops`、`World.exchange_needs`）；两种普查都不可少：没有活动的普查，止息可以不息（`World.rest_needs_census`），没有所立者的普查，即使每步都被围住，生起也可以不息（`World.rest_needs_standing`）；每个关系项都体现时，有普查、平衡且耗散的世界从某步起每步被围住就归于静止，不需要判定相等，所以它的生起不息与从某步起每步都被围住不相容（`World.rests_enclosed`、`World.arising_opens`）；八个前提既不决定被围住，也不决定开放：一个满足它们而没有一步被围住的世界以零净量交换、生起不息（`exposed`），满足它们而每步都被围住的互利者归于静止，每个前提都起约束的 `hatch` 也在一次生起与一次止息之后归于静止，但每个前提都起约束并不带来静止：每步一个成员替换另一个的只闭合一次的队列中生起与止息复现（`World.binding_contingent`）；开放连同八个前提也不给生起不息：另一个满足它们而没有一步被围住的世界什么也不生起、不止息（`holding`），所以在有普查、关系项都体现、平衡且耗散的世界中，开放是生起不息的必要条件而不充分（`World.exposed_world`、`World.exposed_standing`、`World.openness_contingent`、`World.hatch_rests`、`World.holding_world`，[continued-change-needs-openness](../results/records/continued-change-needs-openness.md)）。被围住为变化定下界：在有普查、关系项都体现、平衡且耗散的世界中，从第 T 步起每步被围住时，此后任一步活跃的关系项连同自第 T 步以来有生起或止息的步，不多于第 T 步所立者的总水平（`World.enclosure_bounds_change`）；从某步起的变化界就是从第 0 步起的变化界（`changeBound_iff_zero`），变化界之内变化不复现（`ChangeBound.not_recurring`）。被围住的队列（队首只在队内交换，每有成员加入或离开的一步，所立者下降一）每步都被围住，计数从第 0 步起在变化界之内且没有成员加入空队时满足全部八个前提，所以八个前提之下、从某步起每步被围住时，计数的规律恰是计数表、没有关系项活跃的一步上没有生起与从第 0 步起的变化界（`World.enclosed_counting`）；放在其成员曾占的有限多个位置上，这个队列是满足八个前提的有限世界，其中耗散起约束，这个界可以达到（`World.finite_dissipation_binds`、`World.replaced_attains`），所以有限世界中计数的规律相同（`World.finite_counting`、`World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting`）；变化不复现不能加强为一个此后不再生起的步，否则有限全知原则成立（`World.lpo_of_finite_ends`）；在每个前提选择之下，从某步起被围住时，被围住的一步上平衡、耗散与体现就使没有关系项活跃时没有生起（`World.enclosed_binds_counting`），选中这三者时计数的规律再加从某步起的变化界与此后这一条，变化界恰在选中这三者时是规律（`World.enclosed_change_bound_iff`），不同时选中这三者时被围住不加任何规律（`World.enclosed_counting_under`）；有限世界的每一步都被围住，所以在每个前提选择之下，有限世界中计数的规律是每步至多有某数活跃的计数表，在选中平衡与体现、并选中只有活跃者给予或耗散时再加没有关系项活跃的一步上没有生起，在选中平衡、体现与耗散时再加从第 0 步起的变化界：256 个选择在有限世界中给出三种规律；见证把队列的成员依次排在一圈位置上，相距不到一圈的成员位置不同，不立任何东西的环缺体现，每个位置被占时立着的环缺平衡，队首的位置再取用所降、给予所升的环缺耗散（`World.finite_counting_under`、`World.finite_counting_binds_iff`、`World.finite_change_bound_iff`、`Ring.counts`）；有限世界与从某步起被围住的世界恰在选择约束变化且约束计数时有相同的计数规律，否则无界增长的计数，或被围住之前从无到有的生起，由被围住的世界实现，而不由任何有限世界实现（`World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting_under`，[counting-under-enclosure](../results/records/counting-under-enclosure.md)）。所立者的计数受约束得更早：流所取用与给予的是水平，所以在平衡与耗散之下，从第 T 步起每步被围住时，所立者的任何普查自 T 起的变化不多于第 T 步所立者的总水平，被围住而没有关系项立着的一步上没有关系项开始立着，不需要关于活动的前提（`World.enclosure_bounds_standing`、`World.enclosed_binds_standing`）；体现所做的，是把这个界从所立者带到活跃者。所以在每个选择之下，所立者的计数规律在不被围住时就是活动的（不选体现时，由一个始终活跃而不立任何东西的源供给、成员立着而从不活跃的队列满足其余全部前提，`World.sourcedQueue`），从某步起被围住时与有限世界中则是活动的规律在平衡与耗散起约束之处去掉体现：变化界恰在选中平衡与耗散时是所立者的规律，见证是下一个加入者在立着之前就活跃的等待的队列与每个位置始终活跃的环（`World.standing_counting_under`、`World.enclosed_standing_counting_under`、`World.enclosed_standing_bound_iff`、`World.finite_standing_counting_under`、`World.waitingQueue`、`World.activeRing`）。前提不给依存的计数加任何规律：成员在队中时依存于一个立在一、始终活跃的共同条件，各需它一个单位而都被满足，没有什么流动，这样的队列与环满足全部八个前提，实现每个计数表，因为前提只在依存与活动、水平与世系一起时约束它（`World.dependence_counting`、`World.finite_dependence_counting`，[counting-standing-and-dependence](../results/records/counting-standing-and-dependence.md)）。〔定理〕

**三、后续活动的条件**

5. **依存与储备。** 依存跨步形成、维持、断裂与消解。在活动需要支持之下，损失之后的终止、转化与恢复由定理联系起来，构成断裂的那一步选定结局；储备推迟终结而不逃脱（`Becoming.Dependence`、`Becoming.Reserve`）。〔定理〕
6. **更新、补充与给予。** 更新与消耗属于运行，按回程分级，有限的历史不决定分类（`Becoming.Renewal`、`Becoming.Return`、`Becoming.Horizon`）。不息的生成需要更新复现；更多需要没有最终界的补充与补充者（`Becoming.Generation`、`Becoming.Flow`）。平衡的流中每次上升都被给予，体现的生起就是开始作为条件而在，所以生成本身就是补充（`Becoming.Roles`）。在平衡与耗散之下，取用复现或成员生起复现的有限家族被围住时，不能从某步起都没有东西从其外输送入；各自在一步所给之内的取用合起来可以超出，更新只由依存于所更新者的形成给予时，使水平低于其需要的联合超出终结更新者与以这样的需要依存的每个形成，取用者以这样的需要依存时也在内（`Becoming.Continuation`）。一个部分对接下来能形成者的贡献，由有它与无它的比较给出：关闭需要在所需条件上的净汲取，打开需要净给予；以包含比较，部分扩大、缩小、保持不变或混合；支持只从被支持者获得，没有外来给予时终结是吸收态；区域越小，其外越多（`Becoming.Admission`、`Becoming.Regions`，§2）。〔定理〕
7. **同一与整体。** 跨步的同一由命名的模式承载，标签不承载任何东西；普查定律在每个延续关系下分两半成立：没有分裂时止息复现需要生起复现，没有合并且计数有界时生起复现需要止息复现，两者都没有时平衡精确，同一个展开读作萌出或合并时各有一半失效（`Census.balanceFrom`、`Tandem`，[census-balance-under-continuation](../results/records/census-balance-under-continuation.md)）；跨一步，更多迫使生起恰当没有形成延续为两个（`forces_iff_noSplit`）；整体恰在某个部分活跃时活跃，部分短暂的整体只经由部分的生成而持续（`Becoming.Identity`、`Becoming.Wholes`）。〔定理；同一的读法是表示〕

**四、分化**

8. **演化。** 世系由参与、给予与开始作为条件而在定义。只有活跃者给予且活动需要支持时，只有被支持的形成留下后代，传下来的所需至多比给予之步条件的水平多传递的界，界为零时曾在那里被满足（`Slack`），其间的活动设定后来的选择（`Becoming.Evolution`）。传递是前提；演化之论的独立纲领保持原范围（§2）。〔定理〕
9. **认识。** 一个探究的所见范围由它实际执行可达的配置给出；不确定下的判断是一步探究（`Becoming.Inquiry`）。知识的完整说明见第 3 节。〔定理〕
10. **评价。** 结构上，每个事件都活（逐步读法中不侵蚀）是空间的一个部分，它跨越生起与止息，不与其中任何一个重合（`World.living_crosses`、`World.living_crosses_ceasing`；不活的一边在对终结之步汲取承担自身者的判定之外，`World.grazing_world`，但被侵蚀的依存者在该步终结，`World.grazing_browser_ends`：活动需要支持时，对持续不变的依存的侵蚀使依存者在该步之后不活跃，依存者原本活跃时这就是它的终结（`Web.Dependent.ceases_below`），原本休眠时没有终结（`World.seedbank_world`）；这样的侵蚀是否计入不活由 Ren 选择）。规范上，G0 说整体中的生及其继续生成有基本的正面规范意义；它是一个条件，不是公理：在连通整体上，G0 等价于种子加两项中立（`significant_iff`），动力学不决定它（`Web.significance_undetermined`）；比较 C 的排序随读法而变（`Becoming.Ranking`）；条件经活的使用而连通（`Becoming.Correspondence`）；以之为生与世系是两种候选的直接关系（`Becoming.Descent`）。Ren 的重述提议（§6.7）：想要自身延续者必须计入的范围由上面的动力学推出；沿依存，中心不由它推出（`Clearing.center_not_derived`）；每个活的主张都被某个立场持有时，G0 恰等于每个立场的 S0 加立场中立（`significant_iff_held`），在连通、每个立场都持有活主张的整体上，S0 加 N_rel 是另一种补全（`significant_of_transmits`）；AI 把立场中立读作提议的唯一公理，待 Ren。〔G0 不是公理（Ren 2026-10-11 折叠）：它的刻画是不用公理的定理，上面的规律不决定它是否成立，在第二部分的公理之下它是定理；其余是定理；读法的选择属于 Ren；重述待 Ren 接受〕

**五、地位**

11. **完成了什么，没有完成什么。** 每条都有检查过的陈述或公开的前提；核心不导入核心以外的任何东西，每条源声明的公理报告为空。这是[计划 §9](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md#9-走向完整的生成之论--toward-a-complete-theory-of-becoming) 所说四项可检查内容意义上形式陈述的完整，不是必然性的证明。规律只对这样表示的世界成立，每个生成是否都能这样表示，也是所需根据的一部分。不加实质公理、确立整体中的各规定是同一展开中内在相关的环节，这一演绎仍开放（[根基 §5](BECOMING-FOUNDATION.md#5-completion-audit-and-next-central-inference)）；“生”与“生生”的读法属于 Ren；实际展开是否满足各前提是经验问题。开放项按四类列在第 8 节。〔开放〕

This section states the theory once as a whole: the whole and its laws, then the parts carved by premises, the unity of coming to be and ceasing, the conditions of further activity, and the places of evolution, knowing and evaluation. Items 1–9 and the structural half of item 10 belong to Part I, which uses no axiom and closes with [Part I stated whole](#第一部分的总陈述--part-i-stated-whole); of the normative half of item 10, G0, the content and standing belong to Part I, with no axiom (section 5), and asserting it belongs to Part II, where it is a theorem of the one axiom. Each item is marked as **representation** (how a world is represented: discrete steps, natural-number amounts, a shared index identifying conditions with relata, and decidable determinations wherever they are counted, which classically always holds and, asked of every world, is excluded middle), a **law** (no premise, holds of every world so represented, checked), a **theorem** (holds under disclosed premises, checked), an **axiom** (Part II's one axiom, Ren's proposal; G0 is folded and no longer an axiom), a **reading** (Ren's choice) or **open**.

**I. The whole**

1. **The space.** The whole is every world: relata read with one kind, the flow among them and their web (`World`). Formations and conditions are two roles of one relatum, and the two-kind representation embeds in the one-kind reading (`Becoming.Roles`, `World.ofRoles`). Which condition is which relatum is supplied by the representation's shared index. [representation]
2. **Laws of the whole.** The following hold of every world, with no premise about the unfolding. They are of three kinds: identities by definition, arithmetic identities, and consequences of two-valuedness and finiteness; beyond what the representation itself excludes they exclude no unfolding, so they locate the claim about the whole and are not dynamical content. [law]
   - [identity by definition] Arising and ceasing, a dependence forming and dissolving, and coming to stand and ceasing to stand are one change, coming to be and ceasing to be, read of activity, dependence and standing (`change_readings`).
   - [arithmetic identity] Over any stretch, an amount at the end with the falls between is the amount at the start with the rises between (`over_stretch`).
   - [consequence of two-valuedness and finiteness] With the determinations counted decided, in a finite family coming to be and ceasing to be keep pace, each recurring exactly when the other does (`keep_pace`, `coming_recurs_iff`); in a finite world whose activity is decided, arising recurs exactly when ceasing does (`World.arising_iff_ceasing`); asked of every finite world without decidability, this law is equivalent to excluded middle (`World.law_decides`, `World.law_iff_em`); with excluded middle as a hypothesis it holds of every finite world (`World.law_of_em`); classically every activity is decided.
   - [arithmetic identity] Over any finite family of parents and offspring, the offspring's total divides into the parents' amounts weighted by their offspring and the change in transmission (`price`, the counting form of Price's equation); the weighted total is not the selection term proper (`selected_uniform`), and the selection term proper, the covariance of offspring number and amount, is not stated.
3. **Premises carve parts.** Activity needing support, balance, only active relata giving, only active relata taking what they draw on, dissipation, embodiment and a reserve carrying activity are each met by some worlds and not by others (`World.contingent`). A census and bounded transmission are premises of worlds too. Among the premises, activity needing support makes activity carried, embodiment makes whatever arises embodied, a finite world whose activity is decided has a census, and with only active relata taking and dissipation only active relata give across an enclosed step, as every step of a finite world is (`World.acting_across`); the remaining eight are independent relative to one another, each failed by a world meeting the other seven (`World.independent`), while finiteness, enclosure and decidability are not among them, and with them only active relata giving and a census follow; the eight are the seven above without the reserve carrying activity, which follows from activity needing support, together with a census and bounded transmission, and with the two weaker forms (a reserve carrying activity, embodied arising) they make ten; one world meets them all, the four about the flow vacuously (`World.mutualism_premised`), and in `hatch` all eight hold and each binds (`World.Binds`, `World.hatch_binds`): a parent takes two units of food and its own unit and gives its young one unit, the young comes to stand depending on the food as the parent did, and the parent ceases; infinitely many ground relata stand at nothing, so the world is not finite, though only the food, the parent and the young are ever active and the ground relata are idle (`World.hatch_ever`, `World.hatch_idle`), a census following from the list of what is ever active (`World.counted_of_ever`): the census binds here only as the world is not finite, not by doing work beyond finiteness; activity needing support binds without being tight (the food never falls below the need), and the four premises about the flow bind at step 0 only. Premises lacking balance or embodiment leave counting free: every tally is the census of a world meeting every premise but embodiment, and of one meeting every premise but balance; balance and embodiment, with only active relata giving, bind it together: what arises is given by a relatum active at the step, so nothing arises across a step at which nothing is active (`World.counting_free`, `World.counting_binds_jointly`); that is all the eight add: an open queue (its front giving to each relatum joining, taking from each leaving and, while active, exchanging a unit each way with every ground relatum) meets all eight wherever nothing joins an empty queue, so under all eight the laws of counting are the tally and this one (`World.premised_counting`, [whole-law-of-counting](../results/records/whole-law-of-counting.md)); in that queue dissipation, activity needing support and bounded transmission hold vacuously: nothing taking part ever takes from a closed family, and nothing depends on anything (`World.openQueue_not_binds`); in a world in which every premise binds, every census counts something arising, which is no law of counting under the eight (`World.binds_arising`, `World.binds_adds`), and that is all binding adds: a queue closed once meets all eight with each binding and counts every tally with something arising and nothing arising across a step at which nothing is active, so where every premise binds the laws of counting are the tally and these two (`World.binding_counting`). Binding means that the condition has an instance: in that queue dissipation does constrain the exchange at the step it closes, while activity needing support and bounded transmission hold whatever is active and whatever flows, and exclude nothing (`OnceClosedQueue.dependent_any_activity`, `OnceClosedQueue.transmits_any_flow`). No finite world binds every premise (`World.Finite.not_binds`); without its idle ground, `hatch` is a finite world meeting the eight in which every premise but the census binds (`World.finiteHatch_binds_but_census`); in finite worlds where every premise but the census binds, the laws of counting under the eight are those of finite worlds and that something arises (`World.finite_binding_counting`). Each premise binds on its own (`World.Premise.Binds`). Under each selection, where each selected premise binds, the laws of counting are those under the selection and at most three more: something is active, exactly where activity needing support, only active relata giving, only active relata taking or embodiment is selected; something arises, exactly where embodiment is; and something arises across a step at which something is active, exactly where embodiment is selected and the selection either binds counting or selects only active relata giving and bounded transmission, the latter making what arises descend at that step from an active relatum (`World.binding_counting_under`, `World.binding_activity_iff`, `World.binding_arising_iff`, `World.binding_joint_iff`). The witnesses saturate the web, every relatum depending on every relatum at what it stands at (`Web.saturated`), so that activity needing support holds whatever is active and, with levels bounded, bounded transmission whatever flows; in a lossy queue the front, across one step, exchanges a unit each way with itself and each member joining and loses one unit more, which no standing records (`World.lossyQueue`); a relay hands one of two units on to a ground relatum and loses the other (`World.relayQueue`). Under each of the 256 selections of the eight premises the laws of counting are of two kinds: the tally alone, or the tally with nothing arising across a step at which nothing is active; the second holds exactly where balance and embodiment are selected, with only active relata giving, or with both only active relata taking and dissipation (`World.counting_under`, `World.counting_binds_iff`). The second route needs no active giver: at a step at which nothing is active nothing is taken, whatever is given comes to stand and is active after the step, the relata active after it form a closed family, and dissipation lets nothing be given to it without taking (`World.taking_binds_counting`); every other selection is met by a queue counting every tally, among them a queue driven by an inactive ground relatum, meeting every premise but only active relata giving and taking (`World.drivenQueue`). Further constraint comes from enclosure: with every step from some step on enclosed, the counts also have a change bound, exactly where balance, embodiment and dissipation are selected (`World.enclosed_change_bound_iff`); finite worlds have the same laws of counting under all eight premises, and under a selection exactly where it bounds change and binds counting (item 4). Enclosure and binding together: with every step from some step on enclosed, where the premises of each selection bind, the laws of counting are those under enclosure and the selection and at most the same three, each added under exactly the same selections as where nothing is enclosed, since what arises may arise before enclosure (`World.enclosed_binding_counting_under`, `World.enclosed_binding_joint_iff`), only that something arises under all eight (`World.enclosed_binding_counting`); no finite world binds the census (`World.finite_binding_census`), and in finite worlds, under each selection without it, where its premises bind, at most the same three are added, the first two under the same selections and the third also where embodiment is selected with balance and dissipation, or with only active relata taking, dissipation and bounded transmission, since every step of a finite world is enclosed and there taking with dissipation gives only active relata giving (`World.finite_binding_counting_under`, `World.finite_binding_joint_iff`, `World.Finite.acting`); the witnesses are finite rings with saturated webs (`World.relayRing`, `World.lossyRing`, `World.exchangingRing`); under enclosure, with balance, embodiment and dissipation but neither only active relata giving nor taking, the witness's front sheds a spare unit at a closed step at which something is active, so that dissipation binds (`World.sheddingQueue`). They are premises, not laws; the theorems below hold on the parts they carve. [theorem]

**II. Coming to be and ceasing in one unfolding**

4. **Within a step, over the long run, and of the whole.** Across a step at which a family is dissipated on, a member rising comes with another member of the family falling (`rise_with_fall`). With no premise about the unfolding, coming to be and ceasing to be keep pace over the long run in a finite family (item 2). In a finite world whose activity is decided, arising recurring and ceasing recurring are one part; only with infinitely many relata do they separate (`World.separate`). What this needs is a bound on the count, not finiteness: where at most B relata are active at each step, listed, the two recur together, with finite worlds and the infinite procession, one relatum active at a time, as cases; the bound may grow between steps at which it returns within some number, while with a census whose count grows without end, however slowly, only ceasing needs arising (accretion, and slow for every non-decreasing, unbounded bound) (`World.CountedWithin.arising_iff_ceasing`, `World.CountedBy.arising_iff_ceasing`, `World.procession_world`, `World.accretion_unbounded`, `World.slow_world`). The count balances exactly: where what holds is listed at each step, over any stretch the list at the end with what ceased is the list at the start with what came, so ceasings exceed comings by at most the count at the start and comings exceed ceasings by at most the count at the end, of activity, standing and dependence alike (`Listed.over_stretch`, `Web.Census.over_stretch`, `Web.StandingCensus.over_stretch`, `Web.DependenceCensus.over_stretch`). Read of recurrence the balance is exact: ceasing recurs exactly when, from each step, some later count falls short of the count at the start with what arose between (`Listed.ceasing_iff_shortfall`); the recurrence laws above all follow from it. The tally is the whole law of counting: across each step some members are kept, with what ceased making the list before it and with what came the list after it (`Listed.keeps`); such counts are exactly the balance with no more ceasing across a step than were listed before it (`tally_iff`), which the balance alone does not give (`balance_not_tally`); every tally is counted by a queue whose relata join at the back and leave from the front (`Queue.counts`), so the counts some listing has are exactly the tallies (`tally_iff_counted`), and a property of the counts holds of every listing exactly when it holds of every tally (`counting_laws`, [whole-law-of-counting](../results/records/whole-law-of-counting.md)). This is the reading of activity, which Ren confirmed is named arising and ceasing (“arising/ceasing is indeed better name”, [words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-arising-ceasing-names.md)). Which parts the proposal's ‘generative’ and ‘degenerative’ name is Ren's to choose: Ren said “generative和degenerate虽然都在一个becoming model上，但是他们不overlap吧” ([words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-renewing-consuming-without-axioms.md#ren-follow-up)); if non-overlap is taken, the activity reading, on which the two coincide in finite worlds, is not what is meant (the AI's inference); read as relations, renewing and consuming formations do not overlap (`Web.renewing_not_consuming`). In a finite, balanced, dissipative world whose activity is decided and whose arising is embodied, neither recurs and the world comes to rest (`World.rests`); so does such a world meeting the eight premises, the proof using balance, dissipation and embodiment among them (`World.premised_rests`); in a balanced flow under dissipation, where embodied members of a finite family arise at arbitrarily late steps, the family is open at arbitrarily late steps and, enclosed around, draws in (`Web.generation_opens`, `generation_draws_in`). The world itself need not be finite; what does the work is finitely many standing and finitely many active at each step, with enclosure around what stands, though in a world that so comes to rest not unboundedly many relata ever stand or act, finiteness being exceeded only by relata that never stand or act (`World.rest_few`): under balance and dissipation the total standing does not rise across an enclosed step and any change of standing lowers it; under a census, recurring ceasing needs recurring arising, and not conversely (`World.arising_of_ceasing`, `World.census_one_way`); so where what stands is listed and arising is embodied, arising stops once every step from some step on is enclosed around what stands (`World.arising_stops`), and with a census of what is active ceasing stops too and the world comes to rest, with finite worlds and worlds whose relata are all embodied as its two cases (the rest theorem `World.comes_to_rest`; `World.Finite.comes_to_rest`, `World.rests_around`); the exchange comes to rest too: across a step enclosed around what stands any taking or giving lowers the total standing, so in a balanced, dissipative world in which what stands is listed, once every step from some step on is enclosed around it no relatum takes or gives at arbitrarily late steps, with no census of what is active and no embodiment, while without any one of enclosure, dissipation, balance and the census of what stands the exchange can recur (`Flow.exchange_lowers`, `World.exchange_stops`, `World.exchange_needs`); both censuses are needed: without the census of what is active ceasing can go on (`World.rest_needs_census`), and without the census of what stands arising can go on though every step is enclosed (`World.rest_needs_standing`); where every relatum is embodied, a world with a census, balanced and dissipative, comes to rest once every step from some step on is enclosed, with no decision of equality, so its arising without end is incompatible with every step from some step on being enclosed (`World.rests_enclosed`, `World.arising_opens`); the eight premises decide neither enclosure nor openness: one world meeting them with no step enclosed exchanges at zero net and keeps arising (`exposed`), and mutualists meeting them with every step enclosed come to rest, as does `hatch`, with every premise binding, after one arising and one ceasing, though every premise binding does not bring rest: in a queue closed once in which one member replaces another at every step arising and ceasing recur (`World.binding_contingent`); nor does openness with the eight premises give arising without end: another world meeting them with no step enclosed never arises or ceases (`holding`), so in a world with a census whose relata are all embodied, balanced and dissipative, openness is necessary for arising without end and not sufficient (`World.exposed_world`, `World.exposed_standing`, `World.openness_contingent`, `World.hatch_rests`, `World.holding_world`). Enclosure bounds change: in a world with a census whose relata are all embodied, balanced and dissipative, with every step from `T` on enclosed, the relata active at any later step, together with the steps since `T` at which something arose or ceased, number at most the total standing at `T` (`World.enclosure_bounds_change`); a change bound from some step is one from the first step (`changeBound_iff_zero`), and within a change bound change does not recur (`ChangeBound.not_recurring`). An enclosed queue (its front exchanging only within the queue, what stands falling by one at each step at which a member joins or leaves) has every step enclosed and meets all eight premises wherever a change bound from the first step holds of its counts and nothing joins an empty queue, so under the eight, with every step from some step on enclosed, the laws of counting are the tally, that nothing arises across a step at which nothing is active, and a change bound from the first step (`World.enclosed_counting`); on the finitely many places its members ever take, the queue is a finite world meeting the eight, in which dissipation binds and the bound is attained (`World.finite_dissipation_binds`, `World.replaced_attains`), so finite worlds have the same laws of counting (`World.finite_counting`, `World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting`); that change does not recur cannot be strengthened to a step after which nothing arises without the limited principle of omniscience (`World.lpo_of_finite_ends`); under each selection of the premises, with enclosure from some step on, balance, dissipation and embodiment leave nothing arising across an enclosed step at which nothing is active (`World.enclosed_binds_counting`), where the three are selected the laws of counting add a change bound from some step with this law from then on, the change bound being a law exactly where the three are selected (`World.enclosed_change_bound_iff`), and enclosure adds no law where they are not all selected (`World.enclosed_counting_under`); every step of a finite world is enclosed, so under each selection of the premises the laws of counting in finite worlds are those of a tally with at most some number active at each step, adding that nothing arises across a step at which nothing is active where balance and embodiment are selected with only active relata giving or with dissipation, and a change bound from the first step where balance, embodiment and dissipation are selected: in finite worlds the 256 selections give three kinds of law; the witnesses seat the members of a queue in turn round a ring of places, members less than a full turn apart at different places, a ring with nothing standing lacking embodiment, one whose places stand while held lacking balance, and one whose front's place also takes what falls and gives what rises lacking dissipation (`World.finite_counting_under`, `World.finite_counting_binds_iff`, `World.finite_change_bound_iff`, `Ring.counts`); finite worlds and worlds enclosed from some step on have the same laws of counting exactly where the selection bounds change and binds counting, and otherwise a count growing without end, or arising from none before enclosure, is counted by an enclosed world and by no finite one (`World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting_under`, [counting-under-enclosure](../results/records/counting-under-enclosure.md)). What stands is bound sooner: what the flow takes and gives is standing, so under balance and dissipation, with every step from `T` on enclosed, the change from `T` of any census of what stands stays within the total standing at `T`, and nothing comes to stand across an enclosed step at which nothing stands, with no premise on activity (`World.enclosure_bounds_standing`, `World.enclosed_binds_standing`); what embodiment does is carry this bound from what stands to what is active. So under each selection the laws of counting what stands are, where nothing is enclosed, those of activity (without embodiment, a queue whose members stand and never act, fed by a source active throughout and standing at nothing, meets every other premise, `World.sourcedQueue`), and with enclosure from some step on and in finite worlds those of activity with embodiment dropped where balance and dissipation bind: a change bound is a law of what stands exactly where balance and dissipation are selected, witnessed by waiting queues, whose next to join is active before it stands, and a ring each of whose places is active throughout (`World.standing_counting_under`, `World.enclosed_standing_counting_under`, `World.enclosed_standing_bound_iff`, `World.finite_standing_counting_under`, `World.waitingQueue`, `World.activeRing`). The premises add nothing to the law of counting dependences: a queue and a ring whose members depend while in it on a common condition standing at one and active throughout, each needing a unit of it and met, with nothing flowing, meet all eight and count every tally, since the premises bear on dependence only together with activity, standing and descent (`World.dependence_counting`, `World.finite_dependence_counting`, [counting-standing-and-dependence](../results/records/counting-standing-and-dependence.md)). [theorem]

**III. Conditions of further activity**

5. **Dependence and reserve.** Dependence forms, holds, breaks and dissolves across steps. Where activity needs support, theorems connect ending, transformation and restoration after a loss, and the step that constitutes the break selects among them; a reserve defers the end without escaping it (`Becoming.Dependence`, `Becoming.Reserve`). [theorem]
6. **Renewal, replenishment and giving.** Renewal and consumption belong to runs, are graded by return, and are not decided by any finite history (`Becoming.Renewal`, `Becoming.Return`, `Becoming.Horizon`). Unceasing generation needs renewal to recur; more needs replenishment and renewers without eventual bound (`Becoming.Generation`, `Becoming.Flow`). In a balanced flow every rise is given to, and embodied arising is coming to stand, so generation is itself replenishment (`Becoming.Roles`). Under balance and dissipation a finite family from which taking recurs, or in which members arise recurrently, enclosed around from some step on, cannot from any step on be fed from nothing beyond it; draws each within what a step gives can together exceed it, and where renewal is given only by formations depending on what they renew, joint excess that brings the level below their need ends the renewers and every formation depending at such a need, a taker among them where it so depends (`Becoming.Continuation`). A part's contribution to what can form next is the comparison with and without it: closing needs a net draw on a condition required and opening a net give; compared by inclusion, a part widens, narrows, keeps the same or is mixed; support is gained only from the supported, and where nothing beyond gives the end absorbs; the smaller the region, the more lies beyond it (`Becoming.Admission`, `Becoming.Regions`, section 2). [theorem]
7. **Identity and wholes.** Identity across steps is carried by the pattern of names, and labels carry nothing; the census laws hold under every continuation in two halves: without splits ceasing recurring needs arising recurring, without merges under a bounded count arising recurring needs ceasing recurring, and with neither the balance is exact, while one unfolding read as budding or as joining defeats one half each (`Census.balanceFrom`, `Tandem`); across one step, more forces arising exactly when no formation continues as two (`forces_iff_noSplit`); a whole is active exactly while some part is, and a whole of brief parts persists only through their generation (`Becoming.Identity`, `Becoming.Wholes`). [theorem; the reading of identity is representational]

**IV. Differentiation**

8. **Evolution.** Descent is defined by taking part, giving and coming to stand. Where only active formations give and activity needs support, only supported formations leave descendants, a transmitted need exceeds its condition's level at the giving step by at most the bound of transmission, and with bound zero was met there (`Slack`), and activity in between sets later selection (`Becoming.Evolution`). Transmission is a premise, and the theory of evolution's own programme keeps its scope (section 2). [theorem]
9. **Knowing.** The scope of an inquiry is the configurations its actual executions reach, and judgment under uncertainty is a one-step inquiry (`Becoming.Inquiry`); section 3 gives the account of knowledge. [theorem]
10. **Evaluation.** Structurally, every event being living, not undermining in the stepwise reading, is one part of the space; it crosses arising and ceasing and coincides with neither (`World.living_crosses`, `World.living_crosses_ceasing`; the corner not living lies outside the verdict about drawing on a self-bearing relatum at its own ending, `World.grazing_world`, but the relatum whose dependence is undermined ends at that step, `World.grazing_browser_ends`: where activity needs support, undermining a dependence persisting unchanged leaves the relatum depending inactive after the step, which is its ending where it was active (`Web.Dependent.ceases_below`) and no ending where it was dormant (`World.seedbank_world`); whether such undermining counts against living is Ren's to choose). Normatively, G0 says that living and further generation within the whole have basic positive normative significance; it is a condition, not an axiom: on a connected whole G0 is equivalent to a seed with two neutralities (`significant_iff`), and the dynamics do not decide it (`Web.significance_undetermined`); what C ranks varies with the reading (`Becoming.Ranking`); conditions connect through their living use (`Becoming.Correspondence`); and living on and descent are two candidate direct relations (`Becoming.Descent`). Ren's proposed restatement (§6.7): the extent a formation wanting its continuation must count follows from the dynamics above, and along dependence the center does not (`Clearing.center_not_derived`); where every living claim is held by some standpoint, G0 is exactly S0 at every standpoint with standpoint neutrality (`significant_iff_held`), and on a connected whole where every standpoint holds a living claim S0 with N_rel is another completion (`significant_of_transmits`); the AI reads standpoint neutrality as the proposal's one axiom, pending Ren. [G0 is not an axiom (folded by Ren, 11 October 2026): its characterizations are theorems with no axiom, the laws above do not decide whether it holds, and under Part II's axiom it is a theorem; the rest are theorems; the choice of readings is Ren's; the restatement awaits Ren's acceptance]

**V. Standing**

11. **What is complete and what is not.** Every item has a checked statement or a disclosed premise; the core imports nothing outside itself and every source declaration has an empty axiom report. This is the completeness of a formal statement in the sense of the four checkable criteria of [plan section 9](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md#9-走向完整的生成之论--toward-a-complete-theory-of-becoming), not a proof of necessity. The laws hold of worlds so represented, and whether every becoming can be so represented is part of the warrant still needed. The deduction that the whole's determinations are internally related moments of one unfolding, without added substantive axioms, remains open ([foundation §5](BECOMING-FOUNDATION.md#5-completion-audit-and-next-central-inference)); the readings of 生 and 生生 are Ren's; whether actual unfoldings meet the premises is empirical. Section 8 sorts what is open into four classes. [open]

## 0. 前提卡 / Premise card

每个前提都具名、可见。在 Lean 中，它们是定义或假设，不是公理；脊柱的每条源声明的公理报告都为空。

Every premise is named and visible. In Lean they are definitions or hypotheses, never axioms; every source declaration of the spine has an empty axiom report.

| 层 / Layer | 前提 / Premise | 内容 / Content | Lean | 地位 / Standing |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 逻辑 / Logic | 证明语言 | Lean 4.29.1 内核与 Std；有限归纳的轨迹 | — | 方法承诺，另作说明 |
| 动力学 / Dynamics | 过程 | 声明可容许的变化：`step s a t` | `Generative.Process` | 每次应用外给的局部规律 |
| 对应 / Correspondence | 可观测量 | 主张的实现、条件水平、依存与汲取对应实际历史 | `Frame.realized`、`Conditions` | 应用义务 |
| 对应 | 形成 E | 被评价的生活与关系确实在展开中形成 | — | 应用义务 |
| 对应 | 连通 | 活的主张经已形成的关系相连 | `Valuation.Connected` | 在条件与参与的对应纪律下由共同条件给出（§6.3）；一条纪律已陈述（交互：一方汲取、另一方以正水平依存），在声明的类中给出连通 |
| 对应 | `living` 的读法 | 已定义三种：整段（活跃且在该运行中不消耗）、逐步（活跃且不侵蚀）、关系层；都相对于一次运行，读出的是与他者依存相容的活动，不含继续生成 | `Conditions.valuation`、`Web.valuation`、`Web.relations` | 公开的读法选择，由 Ren 决定（§6.3）；是否即生生，是哲学论证 |
| 对应 | 持有 | 每个活的主张在某个立场的自身区域中；逐步读、每个形成以自身事件为自身区域时恒成立；无人持有的生命经已形成的关系到达；持有可判定是表示的逻辑承诺 | `Valuation.Held` | Ren 2026-10-10 提议中“想要延续者”的形式读法（AI 的读法）；S0 是否即“想要”的读法待 Ren（§6.7） |
| 伦理 / Ethics | **一条公理**（Ren 提议，第二部分） | 对任何东西，其延续在每一步从每个立场都有意义（Ren：“for anything, its continuation into the future has significance”）；延续之意义的边界中立 | `Unrestricted` | 第二部分的公理（P39，§6.8）：G0、种子、S0、N_rel 与 N_std 由它推出，C 在水平路径上推出；读法是 AI 的，待 Ren；G0 的每处用法由它解除，它就是对任何东西的 G0（P44，§6.12），Ren 2026-10-11 据此把 G0 折叠，G0 不再与它并列为公理（下一行）；不用它时，路线 (b) 到达自身及其所依存的（P42 更正：原写“自身与所在的系统”），只在整体是一个供给闭包时到达整体（P40，§6.9） |
| 伦理 | **G0**（已折叠，不是公理） | 整体中的生及其继续生成具有基本正面意义 | `G0`、`Valuation.Significant` | 原为 Ren 授权采用的公理；Ren 2026-10-11 把它折叠（[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-fold-g0.md)）。第一部分把它作为条件陈述，不用公理：其内容由下面三条精确刻画（`significant_iff`、`significant_iff_held`），动力学不决定它（`Web.significance_undetermined`，§5）。第二部分中它是那一条公理的定理（§6.8），其各处用法由读作已实现的活的内容之意义的正面相关解除（§6.12）；这一读法是 AI 的，待 Ren |
| 伦理 | S0 | 一个立场自身活的、继续生成的内容对它有正面意义 | `SelfGood` → `Seed` | 规范前提；AI 把 Ren 2026-10-10 的重述提议读作：S0 即想要自身延续，每个想要之点都是中心（§6.7），待 Ren；S0 只涉及活的主张，逐步读时已含“不损”的过滤；在第二部分的公理之下处处成立，不需要“想要”（§6.8） |
| 伦理 | N_rel | 意义沿已形成的关系传到活的内容（承受者一侧） | `transmits` | 边界中立前提；在第二部分的公理之下成立（§6.8） |
| 伦理 | N_std | 活的内容的意义对每个立场成立（立场一侧） | `shares` | 边界中立前提；AI 把它读作 Ren 2026-10-10 重述提议的唯一公理：从任一中心有意义的，从每个立场都有意义，没有哪个中心特殊；每个活的主张都被持有时，G0 恰等于每个立场的 S0 加 N_std（§6.7）；它不是唯一的补全（在连通、每个立场都持有活主张的整体上 S0 加 N_rel 亦可）；待 Ren。P39 依 Ren 后来的回答把唯一公理读作不受限的意义，N_std 由它推出（§6.8） |
| 伦理 | A | 有通达、且在所见范围上确保本立场下的正面内容，即有一项支持理由（受话者一侧） | `articulates` | 外在论表述前提；在第二部分的公理之下须读在活的内容上，以裸意义读则给任何新内容以理由（§6.12） |
| 实践 / Practice | C | 无损失且实现更多活的内容的扩展更好 | `Comparison` | 比较前提；在水平路径上，“更好”读作保留且增加有意义者时，由第二部分的公理推出（§6.8）；以可能性读，即公理的比较，它把过度汲取者的不在场排在上面而不扣下事件（§6.12） |
| 实践 | L | 结果比较提升到多结果回应；保守默认是“每个结果都优于每个结果” | `LiftBetter` | 实质承诺（§6.5） |
| 实践 | J1–J4 | 完整比较与保留损失；基本条件优先；有界责任；正当可行的选择 | `PracticalJudgment`、`BetterAt` | AI 提出、Ren 授权的附加承诺 |

**不假定的内容。** 本理论不假定以下各项：

- 生成是好的；
- 整体必然改善或普遍增长；
- 存在固定的共同效用；
- 存在彼此独立的价值所有者；
- 每次变化都具生成性；
- 每个处境都有确定的全局因果次序。

**Not assumed:**

- that becoming is good;
- inevitable improvement or universal growth;
- a fixed common utility;
- independent owners of value;
- that every change is generative;
- a definite global causal order at every interface.

## 第一部分：不用公理 / Part I: Without axiom

Ren 写道：“let's aim for the strongest claim without axiom, as the first part, and add one axiom, as the following part, the no axiom part is most important and elegant”（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-zero-axiom-part-and-names.md)）。第一部分（§1–§5）不用公理，伦理的与实质的都不用。前提只作为条件定理的假设出现；表示的承诺另行公开（理论的完整陈述、§0）。它收束于[第一部分的总陈述](#第一部分的总陈述--part-i-stated-whole)。

Ren wrote: “let's aim for the strongest claim without axiom, as the first part, and add one axiom, as the following part, the no axiom part is most important and elegant”. Part I (sections 1–5) uses no axiom, ethical or substantive. Premises appear only as hypotheses of conditional theorems, and the commitments of the representation are disclosed separately (the theory stated in full, section 0). Part I closes with [Part I stated whole](#第一部分的总陈述--part-i-stated-whole).

## 1. 生成：同一展开 / Becoming: one unfolding

**机制。** 起点是过程：一个配置经一次可容许的变化成为另一个配置。

- 框架 `Frame` 只多加一件事：每个配置上实现了哪些主张。主张描述分化了的实现及其关系，不是独立的所有者。
- 运行 `Run` 是无穷的实际执行。
- 可达 `Reach` 是有限的可容许路径。
- 不变量 `Invariant` 是每步都保持的性质。
- 新实现 `Novel` 是某种读法下此前未实现的主张。

这些都由核心的轨迹 `Trace` 定义，因此与核心的组合、乘积和传递定理直接相接（[Becoming.Frame](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Frame.lean)）。

**Mechanism.** The starting point is the process: a configuration becomes another through an admissible change.

- A `Frame` adds only which claims are realized at each configuration.
- A `Run` is an infinite actual execution.
- `Reach` is a finite admissible path.
- An `Invariant` is preserved by every step.
- `Novel` is a claim of a given reading realized and not realized before.

All are defined through the core `Trace`, so they compose with the core's theorems.

**形成、维持、转化、退化与消解都是沿运行的关系变化，不是五个阶段。** 同一次活动可以使某种继续成为现实能力，同时毁掉另一种条件。一个形态还在，不等于它仍有原来的作用能力。以下结果说明这些区分：

- **端点可能。** 沿一段实际轨迹，全部端点可能得到保留，当且仅当存在回程；否则有构造性的丧失见证（[endpoint-loss-and-return](../results/records/endpoint-loss-and-return.md)）。
- **当前能力与最终可达。** 二者不同：准备会改变一步内的共同实现能力（[effective-capacity-and-eventual-reachability](../results/records/effective-capacity-and-eventual-reachability.md)）。
- **整体可能空间。** 合并一族过程的步骤，恰好保留它们端点历史的并集，当且仅当这个并集对共同边界的接合封闭。包容全部结果，不等于可以任意拼接结果的成立条件（[becoming-context-coherence](../results/records/becoming-context-coherence.md)）。
- **共同作用。** 各部分的局部状态都不变，共同作用仍可改变（[joint-correlation-reduced-dynamics](../results/records/joint-correlation-reduced-dynamics.md)）。局部区别可以丧失，而在保留的共同关系上仍可恢复（[control-coherence-loss](../results/records/control-coherence-loss.md)）。
- **规律与轨迹。** 步骤规律与端点轨迹相互决定（[trace-law-equivalence](../results/records/trace-law-equivalence.md)）；保持步骤的映射传递轨迹（[finite-trace-transport](../results/records/finite-trace-transport.md)）。
- **不加前提的规律。** 以下对每个展开成立，不加任何关于它的前提（[Becoming.Change](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Change.lean)，[coming-and-ceasing-keep-pace](../results/records/coming-and-ceasing-keep-pace.md)）。一个规定跨一步开始成立或不再成立；形成的生起与止息、依存的形成与消解、条件开始与不再作为条件而在，是这同一种变化分别读于活动、依存与水平。任一段步上，一个量在终点加上其间的下降，等于在起点加上其间的上升。量不低于零，所以有限家族中下降复现就需要上升复现：每个展开中，有限多个条件持续下降就持续上升；量有界时，上升复现也需要下降复现。于是在有限家族中开始成立与不再成立同步：任一段步上，终点成立的成员连同其间不再成立的，恰是起点成立的成员连同其间开始成立的，两者之差不超过成员数，一方复现恰当另一方复现。形成有限多且活动可判定时，生起复现恰当止息复现（某个形成在任意晚的步生起，恰当某个形成在任意晚的步止息）；条件开始作为条件而在复现，恰当不再作为条件而在复现，这不需要任何前提。有限性起作用：无尽的新可以只生起而不止息（`Accretion`），无尽的初始可以只止息而不生起（`Attrition`）。下面各条都写明它们加的前提，前提划出整个空间的一部分。表示的方法承诺另行公开：离散的步、自然数的量、计数时的可判定性。
- **依存的变化。** 依存本身属于展开：一个形成依存于哪些条件、所需多少，可以跨步形成、维持、断裂与消解，变化总发生在某一步上（[Becoming.Dependence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Dependence.lean)）。在形成只在有支持处活跃的前提（`Dependent`，排除缓冲与滞后）下，定理把损失之后的三种结局联系起来，它们既不穷尽也不互斥：以不弱于先前的依存继续，意味着条件已恢复；无回程的损失之后仍活跃，意味着依存已转化；依存不变则终止。同一过程、同一配置下，在同一步断裂同一依存（按步首约定读）之后，三种结局都可实现：断裂的读法不决定后继配置，构成断裂的那一步选定结局（[becoming-dependence-dynamics](../results/records/becoming-dependence-dynamics.md)）。`Dependent` 恰是零储备：储备在形成不被支持时不上升、在其间活跃时下降，有储备时形成越过支持至多再活跃其储备那么多次，损失与共灭之后的终结推迟而不逃脱，储备衰减时共灭沿任意长的依存之链逐环有界（食物链这样的有限链在内），不衰减时以排中律为假设，链上每个关系项仍终结而没有界；储备可以就是所持条件的水平，同一展开也可以读作依存转化到所持的条件上（[Becoming.Reserve](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Reserve.lean)，[reserve-defers-ending](../results/records/reserve-defers-ending.md)）。这是表示内的定义与约束，不是第一句话的证明。
- **跨步的同一。** 一步的哪个形成延续前一步的哪个，由表示的命名规定，每一步的关系并不确定它（[Becoming.Identity](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Identity.lean)，[identity-across-steps](../results/records/identity-across-steps.md)）。改名保持由关系定义的读法（检查了五种）：标签不承载任何东西，名字跨步的模式承载同一。每一步的关系、每个条件的类、取用者的数目与“更多”不依赖跨步的同一；形成在整段上的类、依存的任期与侵蚀、转化与“一个终结而另一个形成”之分、生起与不息依赖它。只改动谁延续谁，这些就全部改变（`Turnover`）；单由命名造成的生起可以满足下文“继续生成”的全部前提（`TideFlip`）。以旧命名为参照保持跨步持续活动与持续依存的名字，改认不制造生起，也不去掉侵蚀；新命名仍可把终结者与在该步或其后开始者接起来，即接续：谱系的成员分开命名时不息地生起，合为一个时没有生起（`Lineage`）；后继者接续前任者时，前任者失去之后的汲取每一步都侵蚀（`Succession`）。双向保持连续时，生起与侵蚀恰被保持，单步的接续被排除（`no_join`），但跨过休眠的接续仍改变形成的类（`Dormancy`）。谱系与成员是同一展开的两个层次；部分与整体的一种关系由整体陈述：整体恰在某个部分活跃时活跃，这是活动的重合，不是构成，谱系是其成员的整体（[Becoming.Wholes](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Wholes.lean)，[whole-persists-through-generation](../results/records/whole-persists-through-generation.md)）。从某步起每步活跃的整体，若部分短暂（从不在相隔 d 步的两步都活跃），或部分终结且同时活跃的部分有限（活动都终结且活跃的部分可有限列出），部分就不断生起：整体经由部分的生成而持续；部分短暂时这可以是同样的部分反复生起（`Layered`），部分终结时是不断新的部分（`needs_new`），都不是数目增长；有永不终结的部分或无穷的同时储备时则不然（`Immortal`、`Countless`），部分不息地生起也不使整体持续（`Gaps`）。接续怎样读、“生生不息”读到哪个层次，属于 Ren。在普查、`present` 不重复与 Taking 之下，由“更多”迫使的不息在每种一对一的命名下成立（`More.unceasing_reidentified`），在每种没有形成延续为两个的延续关系下更多也迫使生起（`More.unceasingFrom`，合并允许）。没有分裂是充分条件，不是必要条件：一次分裂不必使生起失效（`Fork`），把新形成读作萌出时结论不成立（`Budding`）；没有普查时，没有分裂也不够（`Dormant`）。跨一步，条件是精确的：不论步前与步后是哪些形成，更多迫使生起恰当没有形成延续为两个；在每个没有分裂的延续下迫使生起，恰当步后的多于步前的（`forces_iff_noSplit`、`forced_iff_more`）。普查定律也不依赖名字规定的同一：延续下，一个形成止息，若步后没有活跃的形成延续它；在普查之下，没有分裂时步后活跃的连同止息的至多是步前活跃的连同生起的，没有合并时至少如此，两者都没有时恰好相等；所以没有分裂时止息复现需要生起复现，没有合并且计数有界时反之。同一个每步两个形成的展开，读作每个形成只延续自身时每步一个生起、一个止息，读作萌出时止息复现而从不生起，读作合并时生起复现而从不止息（`Census.balanceFrom`、`Tandem`，[census-balance-under-continuation](../results/records/census-balance-under-continuation.md)）。
- **一个关系项的两种角色。** 形成与条件是同一个关系项的两种角色，不是两类存在（[Becoming.Roles](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Roles.lean)，[life-as-condition-of-life](../results/records/life-as-condition-of-life.md)）。两类的表示嵌入没有形成作为条件的一类读法：这样读，列出的读法与估值的连通都不变，其中曾活跃的形成既不体现（恰在活跃时作为条件而在）也不承担自身。一类读法另需一个对应：哪个条件就是哪个形成。它由共同的索引给出，是表示的前提，两类读法检查不到它（`relabel`）；体现与承担自身是在这一对应下的读法。在活动需要支持（`Dependent`）之下，依存于体现关系项的形成只在它活跃时活跃，活动沿依存链传递，一个关系项的终结终结每个依存链到达它的形成（`coextinct`）。体现的关系项的生起就是它作为条件的水平的上升，所以生成本身就是补充（[generation-is-replenishment](../results/records/generation-is-replenishment.md)）。

**Formation, persistence, transformation, degeneration and dissolution are relational changes along runs, not five stages.** One activity can make a continuation actually possible while destroying another condition, and a retained form need not retain its efficacy. The results above establish, at their scopes:

- laws with no premise, holding of every unfolding: arising and ceasing, forming and dissolving, coming to stand and ceasing to stand are one change read of activity, dependence and standing; over any stretch an amount at the end with its falls is the amount at the start with its rises; in a finite family recurring falls need recurring rises, and coming to be and ceasing to be keep pace, so that with finitely many formations and activity decided arising recurs exactly when ceasing does, while ever new or endlessly many formations escape this (`Accretion`, `Attrition`); every law below names the premise that picks out its part;
- endpoint loss and return;
- the difference between current capacity and eventual reachability;
- the exact condition under which pooling processes invents no history;
- joint change with unchanged local states;
- local loss recoverable within a retained joint relation;
- dependence that forms, holds, breaks and dissolves at steps, and three outcomes of a loss (ending, transformation, restoration), connected by theorems where a formation is active only where supported; the reading of the break does not fix which follows; that premise is the zero reserve, and with a reserve, which can be the standing of a held condition, these endings are deferred, not escaped, and under a decaying reserve coextinction runs down a chain of dependence of any length, a finite one such as a food chain included, within a bound at each link, and without decay, with excluded middle as a hypothesis, still runs down it with no bound;
- identity across steps: labels carry nothing, while the pattern of names across steps carries identity; each step's relations, each condition's class and more without end do not depend on who continues whom, while a formation's class, tenure, undermining, transformation and arising do; continuity read one way leaves succession open, read both ways it still leaves succession across dormancy, and the reading of succession is Ren's; under a census, with each formation taking part listed once and only active formations taking, more forces arising under every continuation without splits, a sufficient condition over a run and not a necessary one, while across one step no split is exactly the condition; the census laws hold under every continuation in two halves, ceasing recurring needing arising recurring without splits and, under a bounded count, arising recurring needing ceasing recurring without merges, and one unfolding read as budding or as joining defeats each half. A whole is active exactly while some part is, a coincidence of activity rather than constitution; a persisting whole of brief parts, or of parts that end with finitely many active at once, persists only through their generation: the same parts arising again, or ever new parts, though not more of them.
- formations and conditions are two roles of one relatum: the two-kind representation embeds in the one-kind reading in which no formation is a condition and keeps every listed reading, while which condition is which relatum is supplied by the shared index; where a relatum stands as a condition exactly while it is active, a formation depending on it lives only while it does, and its ending ends every formation whose chain of dependence reaches it; the arising of such a relatum is its coming to stand, so generation is itself replenishment, a requirement that follows standing, not activity.

**整体与 manifold。** 演绎必然性的主张针对 Becoming 整体；生成性是其中的部分，不是每次变化必须满足的条件。退化、终止与混合变化都在同一展开中，不需要被改名为生成。详见[根基范围](BECOMING-FOUNDATION.md#whole-and-manifold)与[关系怎样变化](THEORY-OF-BECOMING.md)。

**整体与其部分。** 空间是每个世界：以一类读法的关系项、其间的流与依存网（[Becoming.Space](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Space.lean)，[space-and-its-parts](../results/records/space-and-its-parts.md)）。不加前提的规律对每个世界成立。七个前提（活动需要支持、平衡、只有活跃者给予、只有活跃者取用其所汲取的、耗散、体现、承载活动的储备）每一个都有满足与不满足它的世界，所以前提划出真部分，不是隐藏的规律；前提的地图把储备换为普查与有界传递，得八个彼此独立的前提。在活动可判定的有限世界中，生起复现恰当止息复现，两者是同一部分；只有关系项无限多时，两者才分离（`Accretion`、`Attrition`），而且须同时活跃者的计数趋于无穷：在任意晚的步至多 B 个活跃时，即使关系项无限多，两者也同步（[bounded-census-keeps-pace](../results/records/bounded-census-keeps-pace.md)）；不给可判定性而对每个有限世界要求这一规律，等价于排中律（`law_decides`、`law_iff_em`）。“每个事件都活”的部分跨越生起与止息：有限世界中，生起复现与否、止息复现与否，都既可以每个事件都活，也可以有事件不活（`shuttle`、`grazing`、`mutualism`、`drawdown`；`living_crosses_ceasing`）。活动相同的两个世界可以只因各侧是否汲取自身的水平而不同（`shuttle`、`selfDrawing`），但 `selfDrawing` 的不活只在逐步读法对终结之步自我汲取的判定之下成立，这一判定属于 Ren。有限、活动可判定、平衡、耗散且生起者体现的世界归于静止，生起与止息都不再复现，每个前提都不可少；没有平衡的见证每步都有交换，耗散不空（`leaking`）。作者把这读作 Ren“计入整个空间，生成的与退化的”的一种结构（AI 论证）：规律属于整体，前提属于部分，活是其中一个部分，不与生起重合。生起复现与止息复现是活动的读法，英文名称 arising/ceasing 经 Ren 确认（中文为 AI 的译名；[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-arising-ceasing-names.md)）；提议中的“生成的”“退化的”指哪个部分由 Ren 选择。Ren 在 2026-10-09 说，“generative和degenerate虽然都在一个becoming model上，但是他们不overlap吧”（[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-renewing-consuming-without-axioms.md)）；若取不重叠，在有限世界中重合的活动读法就不是其所指（AI 的推论）；读作关系，更新的形成与消耗的形成不重叠（`Web.renewing_not_consuming`）。前提之间的蕴含与其余八个的独立见[前提的地图](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Premises.lean)（[map-of-premises](../results/records/map-of-premises.md)）。使世界归于静止的不是世界有限，而是每步立着的与活跃的有限多，连同从某步起围住所立者；在这样归于静止的世界中，曾经立着或活跃的关系项不是无界多，有限之外只多出从不立着、从不活跃的关系项（`rest_few`）：有活动与所立者的普查、生起者体现、平衡且耗散的世界，从某步起每步被围住所立者时归于静止，有限世界与每个关系项都体现的世界是这一静止定理的两种情形；在普查之下止息复现需要生起复现，反之不然（[ceasing-needs-arising](../results/records/ceasing-needs-arising.md)）；两种普查都不可少（`attrition`；`throughput` 与 `embodiedThroughput` 每步都被围住而生起不息）；这样的世界中交换也归于静止，只需所立者的普查、平衡、耗散与被围住，四者缺一交换都可以复现（`exchange_stops`、`exchange_needs`）。所以在有普查、关系项都体现、平衡且耗散的世界中，生起不息需要不被围住的步；八个前提既不决定被围住，也不决定开放，开放连同八个前提也不给生起不息（`exposed` 与互利者；`holding` 不被围住而什么也不生起；在每个前提都起约束的 `hatch` 中世界也归于静止，在每个前提都起约束而每步替换一个成员的只闭合一次的队列中生起不息，[开放](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Openness.lean)、[计数](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Counting.lean)，[continued-change-needs-openness](../results/records/continued-change-needs-openness.md)）。它不证明整体不需要公理。

**Whole and manifold.** The necessity claim concerns Becoming as a whole. Generativity is part of it, not a condition every change must meet.

**The whole and its parts.** The space is every world: relata read with one kind, their flow and their web. The laws with no premise hold of every world. Seven premises (activity needing support, balance, only active relata giving, only active relata taking what they draw on, dissipation, embodiment, a reserve carrying activity) are each met by some worlds and not by others, so premises carve proper parts and are not hidden laws; the map of premises replaces the reserve with a census and bounded transmission, giving eight independent premises. In a finite world whose activity is decided, arising recurs exactly when ceasing does, so they are one part; only with infinitely many relata do they separate (`Accretion`, `Attrition`), and then only where the count of the active tends to infinity: with at most B active at arbitrarily late steps they recur together even among infinitely many relata; asked of every finite world without decidability, the law is equivalent to excluded middle (`law_decides`, `law_iff_em`). The part in which every event is living crosses arising and ceasing: among finite worlds, arising recurring or not, and ceasing recurring or not, occur both with every event living and with some event not living (`shuttle`, `grazing`, `mutualism`, `drawdown`; `living_crosses_ceasing`). Two worlds with the same activity can differ only in whether each side draws on its own standing (`shuttle`, `selfDrawing`), but `selfDrawing` fails to be living only on the stepwise verdict about self-drawing at an ending, which is Ren's to choose. A finite, balanced, dissipative world whose activity is decided and whose arising is embodied comes to rest, arising and ceasing no longer recurring, and each premise is needed; the witness without balance exchanges at every step, so its dissipation is not vacuous (`leaking`). The author reads this as one structure of Ren's ‘count all space, generative, degenerative’ (an AI argument): laws belong to the whole, premises to parts, and living is one part, not coinciding with arising. Arising recurring and ceasing recurring are the reading of activity, with the English names arising/ceasing Ren confirmed (the Chinese being the AI's rendering; [words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-arising-ceasing-names.md)); which parts the proposal's ‘generative’ and ‘degenerative’ name is Ren's to choose. On 9 October Ren said, ‘generative和degenerate虽然都在一个becoming model上，但是他们不overlap吧’; if non-overlap is taken, the activity reading, on which the two coincide in finite worlds, is not what is meant (the AI's inference); read as relations, renewing and consuming formations do not overlap (`Web.renewing_not_consuming`). Which premises follow from which, and the independence of the remaining eight, are in the map of premises (`Becoming.Premises`). What brings a world to rest is not that the world is finite but finitely many standing and finitely many active at each step, with enclosure around what stands from some step on; in a world that so comes to rest not unboundedly many relata ever stand or act, finiteness being exceeded only by relata that never stand or act (`rest_few`): a balanced, dissipative world with censuses of what is active and of what stands, whose arising is embodied, comes to rest once every step from some step on is enclosed around what stands, with finite worlds and worlds whose relata are all embodied as two cases of this rest theorem; under a census, recurring ceasing needs recurring arising, and not conversely; both censuses are needed (`attrition`; `throughput` and `embodiedThroughput`, enclosed at every step, keep arising); in such a world the exchange comes to rest too, needing only the census of what stands, balance, dissipation and enclosure, without any one of which it can recur (`exchange_stops`, `exchange_needs`). So in a world with a census whose relata are all embodied, balanced and dissipative, arising without end needs steps not enclosed; the eight premises decide neither enclosure nor openness, and openness with them does not give arising without end (`exposed` and the mutualists; `holding`, never enclosed, never arises; `hatch`, in which every premise binds, comes to rest too, while a queue closed once replacing one member at every step keeps arising; `Becoming.Openness`, `Becoming.Counting`). It does not show that the whole needs no axiom.

## 2. 组织、更新与演化 / Organization, renewal and evolution

**更新与消耗是同一个分类，属于运行。** 经条件的可观测量读出，任一过程的任一运行都是一个关系展开（[Becoming.Renewal](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Renewal.lean)）。

- **更新。** 一个形成是更新的，当它依存或汲取的每个条件，都继续支持每个依存于该条件的形成。
- **消耗。** 一个形成是消耗的，当它汲取的某个条件，使某个依存者从某步起永久失去支持。
- 两类互斥，但不穷尽：波动的情形两者都不是（[relational-renewal-classification](../results/records/relational-renewal-classification.md)）。

**Renewal and consumption form one classification, and it belongs to runs.** Read through observables of conditions, every run of every process is one relational unfolding.

- A formation is renewing when every condition it depends on or draws on keeps supporting every formation that depends on it.
- It is consuming when it draws on a condition some dependent permanently loses.
- The classes are exclusive but not exhaustive: fluctuating cases are neither.

**一条原则约束无更新的继续：从某步起不再上升的可观测量，只能下降有限次。** 以下三条界都是它的情形（`falls_finitely`）：

- 耗竭：每步都降低储备，则没有运行；
- 自然数秩的复现更新：凡非更新之步都降低秩，则更新任意晚地复现；
- 无更新汲取：汲取者的活跃只有有限次。

**分类属于运行，不属于配置或形成自身的轨迹。** 同一过程、同一初始配置下，焦点形成每一步都活跃：一次运行在更新之内，另一次消耗维持者的条件，维持者随之终止（`class_belongs_to_the_run`）。因此，持续、成功或增长都不能证明更新（[own_trajectory_does_not_decide](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/RelationalRenewal.lean)）。

**逐步读法。** 分类也可以逐步读：一个事件（形成，步）在更新之内，当它所在之步维持它所参与条件上的每个依存（`RenewingAt`）。整段更新的形成，每个活跃步都在更新之内；一个形成也可以在自己的每个活跃步都在更新之内，整段却不更新。

作为 `living` 的逐步读法，四次独立审查之后的工作候选（待 Ren 选择）是“活跃且不侵蚀”。事件侵蚀，当它在某步汲取某条件，而该步之后，条件既低于一个依存现在的所需，也低于该依存在不间断成立期间的某步曾被满足的所需（`MetAbove`）。在断裂某个依存之步汲取（断裂）是侵蚀的一种。这一读法：

- 记入被断裂者自己的事件（它不汲取）；
- 在依存成立期间从未以其所需被满足的依存，不使汲取者侵蚀，所需下降时也如此（`NewNeed.never_met_not_undermining`、`FallingNeed.never_met_not_undermining`）；只低于增长到从未被满足的所需，不算侵蚀（`RisingNeed.grown_need_not_met`），所需增长之后，把水平取到曾被满足的所需之下的汲取侵蚀而不断裂（`Stunted.stunting_undermines`）；依存中断一步即开始新的一段（`Gap.lapse_starts_afresh`），休眠或迁出是否表示为持续的依存，是应用义务；
- 扣下损失之后继续的汲取：那样的汲取不再断裂什么；在 `Renewable` 中，它使条件回不来（`drawing_after_loss`）；
- 代价：它读的是共同参与一个未恢复的短缺，不是汲取的效果。`draws` 不带数量，所以恰取更新量、甚至不改变任何水平的汲取也被扣下（`Pool.within_share_undermines`、`Pinned.undermining_without_effect`）。

它与整段读法两个方向都有分歧：加码之前的汲取逐步被记入，整段却记为消耗；短暂下降中断裂之步的汲取逐步不被记入，而条件随后恢复，整段不记消耗（[becoming-stepwise-reading](../results/records/becoming-stepwise-reading.md)，[审查与更正](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md)）。

**回程给更新分级。** 更新与消耗之间按回程时间分级：每步都维持、在有界周期内回到、终会回到而无界、不再回到。消耗恰是某个依存不再回程（`consumed_iff`）。季节性条件在两步周期内更新；恢复可以无界地变慢而条件从不被消耗。更新蕴含一步周期内更新，逆命题不成立（`LateMet`）。周期层面的读法在两步周期下记入短暂下降，对每个周期都扣下加码（[becoming-return-grades](../results/records/becoming-return-grades.md)）。

**有限的历史不决定分类。** 对每个视界，同一过程有两次运行在视界之前完全相同：每步都超出更新地汲取，每个事件都在更新之内；此后一次更新，一次消耗。任何由有限前缀决定、并记入每次更新运行的读法，必记入某次消耗运行（[becoming-finite-horizon](../results/records/becoming-finite-horizon.md)）；在有反馈的支持门控定律中也是如此。这一结论的力量来自“记入每次更新运行”：那次更新运行在视界之前同样超出更新地汲取，整段读法记入这些汲取（`whole_run_credits_overdraw`）；按数量的读法（所取不超过更新）可由有限历史判定，并扣下每一次超出（`amount_reading`）。若要判断的是一段汲取的整段结局，需要对运行之继续的探究与审议，不只是对过去的观察。

**One principle bounds continuation without renewal: an observable that never rises from some step on falls only finitely often.** Its cases are depletion, natural-rank recurrent renewal and unrenewed drawdown. **The class belongs to the run**, not to a configuration or to the formation's own trajectory: persistence, success and growth do not certify renewal. **A stepwise classification** places an event within renewal when its step keeps every dependence on the conditions it participates in. As the stepwise reading of `living`, the working candidate after four independent reviews, pending Ren's choice, an event lives when it is active and not undermining: it does not draw on a condition at a step after which the condition is below both the present need of a dependence on it and a need that dependence met during its unbroken tenure. It and the whole-run reading differ in both directions. **Return grades renewal**: consumption is exactly a dependence that never returns, and recovery can slow without bound while the condition is never consumed. **No finite history decides the classification**: any reading fixed by a finite prefix that credits every renewing run credits some consuming run, with feedback too. A per-step amount reading escapes this only by not crediting every renewing run.

**联合消耗没有单一的消耗者。** 两个形成各自都在更新之内，合起来却消耗了共同条件，两者都随之终止。这一类属于它们的联合关系（`jointly_consuming`）。

- **反馈。** 在更新依赖被更新条件的定律族中，越过支持阈值的损失在定律内不会回来（`no_return_below_support`）。
- **修复。** 有支持的贡献路线可以修复维持关系（[supported-renewal-recovery](../results/records/supported-renewal-recovery.md)）。
- **替代。** 同一定律族中，能替代条件的消耗者转化依存而持续，在整段读法下仍是消耗的（几乎由定义给出），维持者终止；逐步读法在维持者失去支持之前记入它，此后到它转向为止扣下它（`stepwise_credits_substitution`、`slow_withheld_two_steps`）。自身的持续不证明更新（[substitution-preserves-consumption](../results/records/substitution-preserves-consumption.md)）。
- **窄定律类。** 在一个磨损定律类中，更新与消耗是动力学上可区分的（[renewing-consuming-formation](../results/records/renewing-consuming-formation.md)）。

**继续生成。** 以上读法读出的都是“不损”。作者把生生的第二个“生”的一种读法陈述为生起：一个形成跨某步生起，当它此前不活跃、此后活跃；生成不息，当某个形成在任意晚的步生起，同一形成可以再生起（[Becoming.Generation](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Generation.lean)，[unceasing-generation-needs-renewal](../results/records/unceasing-generation-needs-renewal.md)）。一个事件消耗一个有限的条件家族，当在它的每个家族都不上升之步，其中某个条件下降；这只说事件的每一步家族中有条件变化，不涉及事件与那个条件的关系（`depletes_iff`、`Tide.unrelated_depletion`）。若生起消耗家族，不息的生成使其中某个条件在任意晚的步净上升；若每次上升都由某个形成的更新造成，更新的活动就在任意晚的步复现。平衡的流给出不需要净上升的形式：在平衡的流中，任意晚的步上有正的取用，就需要任意晚的步上有补充（`uptake_needs_replenishment`），它看得见与汲取同步的补充；取用的形成不息地生起是其一种情形，补充者于是在任意晚的步出现（`replenishment_recurs`），恒定的取用者群体是另一种（`Steady`）。生起在这一形式中只经由它带来的取用起作用。月亮更新的潮水在任意晚的步上升而没有任何生起，所以在这些前提下，不息的生成严格强于复现的更新（`Tide.renewal_without_arising`）。以不被更新的条件为条件的生起停止；在有限个不被更新的条件之间替代，推迟终结而不逃脱终结。一个量从不上升而每次生起都使它下降（耗散）时，生成不是不息的（`dissipation_ceases`）；没有耗散，总量不变的封闭循环可以维持不息的生成（`Recycle.closed_unceasing`）。作者由此论证：在耗散之下，任何有限部分都不能独自不息；对流中的取用，证明的是它的字面形式：取用复现的有限家族在任意晚的步不封闭，家族被围住时须从其外汲入（见下文“更多”）；耗散空成立时，有限部分可以独自承担，而开放由与它无关的交换满足（`SprayShuttle`）。这里只读出 Ren 原话“不断创造更多生”中的“不断…生”；第二个“生”的范围留给 Ren。

**Continued generation.** As the author's reading of the second 生, a formation arises across a step when it is inactive before and active after it; generation is unceasing when some formation arises at arbitrarily late steps. An event depletes a finite family when at each of its steps at which none of them rises one of them falls, which says only that some condition of the family changes. When arising depletes the family, unceasing generation makes renewal recur; in balanced flows, uptake at arbitrarily late steps needs replenishment at arbitrarily late steps, which sees replenishment concurrent with drawing; takers arising unceasingly are one case and a constant population of takers another, so arising works there only through the uptake it brings. A tide the moon renews rises with nothing arising, so under these premises unceasing generation is strictly stronger than recurring renewal. Arising on a consumed condition ceases, and substitution among unrenewed conditions defers the end without escaping it. Under dissipation generation is not unceasing; without it, a closed cycle can be. Only the “unceasing” of Ren's “unceasingly creating more 生” is stated here; the scope of the second 生 is Ren's.

**更多。** 把条件读作数量：每步参与的形成从条件取用多少、给予多少（[Becoming.Flow](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Flow.lean)、[Becoming.Population](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Population.lean)，[more-needs-renewers](../results/records/more-needs-renewers.md)）。流平衡时，条件水平的每次变化恰是取用与给予之差；一步或一段步上，家族的总水平加上取用，等于之前的总水平加上补充（消耗与补充的平衡）。每次上升于是都由某个活跃的补充者造成，上面“每次上升都由形成的更新造成”从前提变为定理。“更多”读作（作者的读法）：对每个数，从某步起至少有那么多形成从一个有限的条件家族取用。量是自然数，每个取用者每步至少取用一个单位，所以这样的生成需要家族所得的补充没有最终的界；每个形成每步的给予有上限时，补充者也没有最终的界，在平均的意义上越来越多，却可以在无穷多步上为零（`Pulse.pulsed`）。在活跃形成的普查之下，流的 `present` 每步每个形成只列一次，只有活跃的形成取用时，不止地更多蕴含不息的生成（`More.unceasing`）；扩张与循环满足全部这些前提（`Expansion.counted`、`Cycle.counted`）；不息地反复生起的振荡者满足这些前提而不更多，所以蕴含是严格的；没有普查、`present` 有重复、或取用者从不活跃时，不止地更多可以没有任何生起（`Dormant`、`Repeated`、`Inactive`）。所需的补充于是构成严格的层级：复现的取用需要补充在任意晚的步出现，不止地更多需要补充没有最终的界。补充者可以就是取用者自身（`Cycle.closed_more`）。在耗散之下（每一步，每个在该步封闭的不重复有限家族上，参与的形成给予它的不多于从中所取，取用时更少），取用在其上复现的有限家族，连同包含它的每个有限家族，在任意晚的步都与其外交换，这一交换可以与家族无关；家族被围住（每步的交换落在包含它的有限个条件之内）时，它汲入：在任意晚的步有形成给予它而从其外取用（`taking_draws_in`）；条件总共有限时，取用不再复现（[Becoming.Dissipation](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Dissipation.lean)，[no-finite-part-bears-taking](../results/records/no-finite-part-bears-taking.md)）。在平衡的流中，每次下降都被取用；被耗散的一步上，家族成员的下降至少等于其上升，每个取用的形成再多一。所以一个成员上升（包括体现的生起），同一步上另一个成员下降，两者是同一次交换的两面；不重复的家族中有上升而没有下降，就表明家族在该步不封闭；没有耗散时不然（`Brood.rise_without_fall`）。对各步家族被耗散且被取用的事件，上面作为前提的消耗由此推出（[rising-and-falling-in-one-exchange](../results/records/rising-and-falling-in-one-exchange.md)）。下降不是止息，也不是侵蚀。耗散只约束有封闭的有限家族的步：在这样的步上，给予须以取用为条件（`giving_needs_uptake`）；没有封闭的步时它是空的，补充者可以什么都不取而给予（`Spray`）。作者把它读作“生生”的一种结构：更多的取用需要没有最终界的补充，由展开之内的形成给出；在耗散之下，复现的取用，无论来自不止的更多、生成还是维持，都由越出每个有限部分的展开承担，维持一个取用者已经如此（`Lone`）。可能性、多样性与复杂性的上升是“更多”的另外读法，这里不陈述。

**More.** Read as amounts, conditions are taken and given by the formations taking part in each step, and depletion balances replenishment. In a balanced flow every rise is made by an active renewer, so the premise above becomes a theorem. Generation more without end, with ever more formations taking from a finite family from some step on (the author's reading), needs replenishment with no eventual bound, since each taker takes at least one unit; when each formation gives at most a fixed amount, renewers have no eventual bound and grow in an averaged sense, though they can be absent at infinitely many steps. Under a census of active formations, with each formation listed once in the flow's present and only active formations taking, more without end implies unceasing generation, strictly, and the expansion and the cycle meet every premise; without the census, with repetition in present, or with inactive takers it does not. The replenishment needed thus forms a strict hierarchy. The renewers can be the takers themselves. Dissipation binds at steps where some finite family is closed, and there giving needs uptake; under it, a finite family on which taking recurs, whether from more, generation or maintenance, and every finite family containing it, is open at arbitrarily late steps, though the opening exchange may be unrelated to it; enclosed around, it draws in, some formation giving to it and taking from beyond it at arbitrarily late steps; and with finitely many conditions in all, taking does not recur. In a balanced flow every fall is taken from.
