Across a step dissipated on, a family's members fall by at least as much as they rise, so a member rising, as an embodied relatum arising, comes with another member falling in the same exchange; a rise with nothing falling shows a family without repetition open across that step; and for events whose family is dissipated on and taken from at each step, the depletion premise above follows. A fall is a lowering, neither a ceasing nor an undermining, and without dissipation a rise need not be accompanied. Diversity, complexity and possibility are not stated.

**一个关系项的两种角色，经由给予的生起。** 以一类关系项读（[Becoming.Roles](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Roles.lean)，[life-as-condition-of-life](../results/records/life-as-condition-of-life.md)），一个形成可以是其他形成与自身的条件；哪个条件就是哪个形成，由表示的共同索引给出（`relabel`）。在平衡且只有活跃形成给予的流中，体现的关系项只经由另一个活跃形成的给予而生起（`Embodied.arises_given`）；给予它的形成不必体现，可以是一个不依存于任何东西的源（`SourceSeed`）。在只有体现的关系项给予这一表示前提下，给予它的关系项也体现（`arises_from_embodied`），它仍可以是一个不依存于任何东西的源（`EmbodiedSource`）。在把体现的关系项读作“生”这一属于 Ren 的读法下，作者把这读作原话中两个“生”之间关系的一种形式内容（AI 论证），不替代 Ren 的原话；一代代形成可以不止地生起，每一代由前一代给予（`Brood`）。体现的关系项在生起时开始作为条件而在，至少被给予它的新水平，所以生成本身就是补充：体现的成员在任意晚的步生起时，家族的补充复现，不论取用什么（[generation-is-replenishment](../results/records/generation-is-replenishment.md)）。这一要求跟着水平的上升走，不跟着活动走：平衡的流中任何上升都被给予（`Balances.rise_given`），开始作为条件而在是从零的上升（`Web.ComesToStand`），一个条件也可以只是不断上升（`Growing`）；种子可以在休眠时被给予它的水平，此后生起时没有任何给予（`Provisioned`），从休眠中醒来不开始作为条件而在，不受这一要求约束（`Sleeper`）；没有支持时，在储备之下醒来只有有限多次，储备推迟终结而不逃脱（[reserve-defers-ending](../results/records/reserve-defers-ending.md)）。给予被耗散家族的形成从家族取用，所以在耗散之下，封闭的不重复家族中体现成员的每次生起都降低家族的存量；体现的生成在一个有限家族中复现，家族就在任意晚的步不封闭，这一交换可以与家族无关（`SprayShuttle`），家族被围住时它汲入（`generation_draws_in`）；关系项总共有限时它终将停止（`generation_opens`、`generation_ceases`）。上文“继续生成”中 `dissipation_ceases` 所设的物理前提，对封闭家族中体现成员的生起，由平衡、耗散与体现推出。没有耗散（`Shuttle`）或不体现（`Flicker`）时，这些结论不成立；燃烧新燃料的传递满足全部前提（`Throughput`）。汲取承担自身的关系项，与它的每个活跃事件相互作用：蜜蜂与花各自依存于自身与对方时，没有第三个条件就连通，G0 在那里恰为种子加两项中立；各自只依存于对方时，两种估值都不关联它们，尽管两者都体现：体现本身不进入估值（`Mutualism`、`Mutualism.apart_embodied`）。在承担自身的体现关系项终结之步汲取它，不论是什么终结了它，是断裂，所以不是 living（`Embodied.ending_breaks`、`Predation`）；这包括旁观的汲取者、自然的终结与该关系项自身的最后事件（`Wilting`）。是否应如此、对哪些汲取者，属于 Ren 对生生的读法。

**Two roles of one relatum, and arising through giving.** Read with one kind of relatum, a formation can be a condition of others and of itself; which condition is which formation is supplied by the representation's shared index. In a balanced flow where only active formations give, an embodied relatum arises only as another active formation gives to it, and the formation that gives need not be embodied (`SourceSeed`). Where only embodied relata give, the relatum that gives is embodied too, and may still be a source depending on nothing (`EmbodiedSource`); taking embodied relata as 生, a reading that is Ren's, the author reads this as one formal content of the relation between the two 生; generations can arise without end, each given by the one before. An embodied relatum comes to stand as it arises and is given at least its new standing, so generation is itself replenishment: where embodied members of a family arise at arbitrarily late steps, its replenishment recurs whatever is taken. The requirement follows the rise of standing, not activity: in a balanced flow every rise is given to (`Balances.rise_given`), coming to stand is the rise from nothing (`Web.ComesToStand`), and a condition can simply keep rising (`Growing`); a seed can be given its standing while dormant and arise later with nothing given (`Provisioned`), and waking from dormancy, which is not coming to stand, is not bound by it (`Sleeper`); without support, under a reserve, waking recurs only finitely often, the reserve deferring the end without escaping it. A formation giving to a family dissipated on takes from it, so under dissipation each embodied arising in a closed family without repetition lowers its stock; a finite family in which embodied generation recurs is open at arbitrarily late steps, though the opening exchange may be unrelated to it (`SprayShuttle`); enclosed around, it draws in (`generation_draws_in`); and with finitely many relata in all it stops. This derives the physical premise of `dissipation_ceases` for such arisings. Without dissipation (`Shuttle`) or without embodiment (`Flicker`) these conclusions fail; a shuttle that burns fresh fuel meets every premise (`Throughput`). Drawing on a relatum that bears on itself interacts with each of its active events, so mutualists each depending on itself and on the other relate directly, with no third condition; where each depends only on the other, neither valuation relates them, though both are embodied. Drawing on an embodied relatum that bears on itself, across the step at which it ends, is breaking and so not living, whatever ends it: this includes a bystander, a natural ending and the relatum's own last event (`Wilting`). Whether it should be, and for which drawers, is Ren's reading.

**演化是具体的机制，不是一切变化的同义词。** 传递、重建、变异与选择各有其关系，须各自说明（[演化之论](THE-THEORY-OF-EVOLUTION.md)、[演化组织](EVOLUTIONARY-ORGANIZATION.md)）。已检查的连接如下：

- 继承的结构排列改变后续复制（[inherited-structural-copying](../results/records/inherited-structural-copying.md)）；
- 形成的登记改变复制的保真度（[formed-copying-registration](../results/records/formed-copying-registration.md)）；
- 形成的检查／编辑改变已形成的后继（[formed-copying-correction](../results/records/formed-copying-correction.md)）；
- 生长约束碎裂与后继组成（[growth-constrained-fragmentation](../results/records/growth-constrained-fragmentation.md)）；
- 组织规则的形成与继承（[organizational-rule-inheritance](../results/records/organizational-rule-inheritance.md)）。

**在脊柱上，经世系的演化有最小的形式内容**（[Becoming.Evolution](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Evolution.lean)，[evolution-on-descent](../results/records/evolution-on-descent.md)）。世系由参与、给予与开始作为条件而在定义，不含估值。不加前提：任意有限的亲代家族与后代，后代的总量分为亲代的量按其后代加权，以及传递的变化（`price`，Price 方程的计数形式；它是算术恒等式；加权总量不是真正的选择项，每个亲代都有 k 个后代时它是亲代总量的 k 倍，`selected_uniform`）。只有活跃者给予且活动需要支持时，只有被支持的形成留下后代（`unsupported_childless`），亲代所需的加权总量受条件的水平所限（`selected_le`）。后代承接亲代的依存（`TransmitsWithin`，前提）时，传下来的所需至多比给予之步条件的水平多传递的界，界为零时曾在那里被满足（`transmitted_supported`、`Slack`）；在平衡的流中，其间的取用与给予设定后来能传递的（`transmitted_within`）。一个亲代自己的取用降低了池，使承继其所需的后代不被支持，所需较少的后代被支持并转而取用（`Drawdown`）：传递、变异、选择与活动对选择的反馈在同一展开中。适应度没有定义；繁殖率、突变、漂变与种群动力学不在其中；上列机制保持各自的范围，与它们的对应尚未证明。

**Evolution names specific mechanisms**, with transmission, reconstruction, variation and selection each explained through their own relations. It is not a synonym for every change. On the spine, evolution through descent has a minimal formal content. Descent is defined by taking part, giving and coming to stand, with no valuation. With no premise, the offspring's total divides into the parents' amounts weighted by their offspring and the change in transmission (`price`, the counting form of Price's equation, an arithmetic identity; the weighted total is not the selection term proper, being k times the parents' total where every parent has k offspring, `selected_uniform`). Where only active formations give and activity needs support, only supported formations leave descendants, and the parents' weighted needs are bounded by the condition's level. Where descendants take up their parents' dependences (`TransmitsWithin`, a premise), a transmitted need exceeds its condition's level at the giving step by at most the bound of transmission, and with bound zero was met there, and in a balanced flow what is taken and given in between sets what can be transmitted later. A parent's own taking can leave the descendant inheriting its need unsupported while one needing less is supported and takes in turn (`Drawdown`). Fitness is not defined; reproductive rates, mutation, drift and population dynamics are not covered; the mechanisms above keep their scope, and no correspondence to them is yet proved.

**接下来能形成什么：接纳、部分与区域（第一部分，P37）。** Ren 2026-10-10 写道，生成的是“its existence creates more possibilities of becoming - more future, not less”，又写道“"not harm" - this is a phase transition with a clear boundary, not some "outside will or belief"”。Ren 确认了两项读法（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)）：
- 可能性是从现有者接下来能形成什么，一个将来保留另一个所有的并有所增加时更多；
- 整体从不是任何中心对它的模型。

以下在脊柱上不用公理陈述这一读法（[Becoming.Admission](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Admission.lean)、[Becoming.Regions](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Regions.lean)）；形式化是 AI 的。

- **接纳与支持的来源。** 水平满足一个形成此时的每项要求，就接纳它；支持是所立水平的接纳。要求不放松时，形成只因所需条件上升而获得支持。在平衡、只有活跃者给予且活动需要支持的流中，每次上升都由被支持的形成给予，所以支持只从被支持者获得（`gains_from_supported`）。没有外来给予、要求不放松时，不被支持的就不再被支持：终结是吸收态，每项前提都必要（`unsupported_stays`、`Restoring`；[support-from-the-supported](../results/records/support-from-the-supported.md)）。
- **相界是净交换。** 扣留一个部分（一个形成或一个系统）时，其余交换照旧，扣留后的水平存在时唯一。差额精确：每个水平加上该部分取走的，等于扣留后的水平加上该部分给予的（`Balances.withheld`）。所以相界是精确的：一个形成被支持，恰当在它所需的每个条件上，所需加上该部分到此为止取走的，不多于扣留后的水平加上该部分给予的，即部分的累计净汲取不超出扣留后的水平满足所需的余量（`supported_iff_within_margin`）。部分关闭一个能形成者，只经由在它所需条件上超出余量的净汲取（找出那个条件要用经典逻辑）；打开只经由净给予（`supported_of_net_give`、`admits_of_net_draw`）。每步给回不少于所取的部分不关闭任何东西，给予不多于所取的不打开任何东西，恰好给回的什么都不改变（[closing-needs-net-draw](../results/records/closing-needs-net-draw.md)）。
- **四种情形。** 以包含比较有它与无它的水平（每一步接纳的形成），部分有四种情形（这一划分用经典逻辑）：
  - 扩大：来自外的给予者（`Inflow.widens`）；
  - 缩小：清除者（`Clearing.narrows`）；
  - 保持不变；
  - 混合：两边各有对方没有的，如输送者（`Transfer.mixed`）。

  混合的部分不为包含定序，定序需要度量，那是另一个前提；包含不许可强加的终结（[four-cases-of-a-part](../results/records/four-cases-of-a-part.md)）。
- **层级。** 在某区域内成立的，在其内每个区域内都成立；在某区域外失去的，在其内每个区域外都失去：区域越小，其外越多；整体之外什么都没有。清除者每步都活跃且被支持，在它与它所依存的来源之内不关闭任何东西；而在其外，栖居者无清除者的部分时被接纳，有时不被接纳（`Clearing.loses_beyond`；[loss-beyond-a-region](../results/records/loss-beyond-a-region.md)）。所以，部分可以延续，同时关闭其依存之外的东西；“只有不退化的动力学能无限延续”对部分不成立，对整体则没有可以推出去的地方。
- **所表示的边界。** 一个形成在一个条件区域上更新，是指它活跃，且该步维持区域中它所参与的每个条件上的每个依存。与实际依存比较（[represented-boundary-against-dependence](../results/records/represented-boundary-against-dependence.md)）：
  - 区域覆盖它参与的每个条件时，这就是该步的更新；
  - 区域覆盖它所需的每个条件、要求不收紧时，它保住自身支持，却仍可侵蚀他者：清除者在其资源上更新，在第一步侵蚀；
  - 区域遗漏一项所需条件时，自身的汲取可以侵蚀自身的依存，失去支持（`Omitted`）。

  Ren 说中心与其边界都在同一认识动力学之内（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-centers-within-the-dynamics.md)）；这里区域只是陈述的参数，没有展开之外的地位。

已有的相界结果仍在 `Becoming.Continuation`：
- 没有条件能被无限耗下去（`excess_bounded`）；
- 使水平低于所需的联合超出终结以其所需依存者（`joint_excess_ends`）。

以下是 AI 的读法，待 Ren：
- 把扩大、缩小、不变读作 Ren 的生成、退化、中性；
- 把区域读作自身、系统与整体，以及中心所表示的边界。

比较的是被接纳的形成，不比较 Ren 也提到的更新能力与关系的丰富。这些定理按强弱排列判据，不说形成应当遵循哪一个。

**What can form next: admission, parts and regions (Part I, P37).** On 10 October 2026 Ren wrote that being generative is that “its existence creates more possibilities of becoming - more future, not less”, and that “"not harm" - this is a phase transition with a clear boundary, not some "outside will or belief"”. Ren confirmed two readings:
- possibilities are what can form next from what exists, one future having more when it keeps all the other has and adds some;
- the whole is never any center's model of it.

The following states that reading on the spine with no axiom; the formalization is the AI's.

- **Admission and where support comes from.** Levels admit a formation when they meet every requirement it has; support is admission by the levels that stand. Where requirements do not loosen, a formation gains support only through a rise of a condition it requires. In a balanced flow where only active formations give and activity needs support, every rise is given by a supported formation, so support is gained only from the supported. Where nothing beyond gives and requirements do not loosen, what is unsupported stays so: the end absorbs, and each premise is needed.
- **The boundary is a net exchange.** A part, a formation or a system, is withheld with the rest of the exchange as it was, and the levels without it are unique where they exist. The difference is exact: each level with what the part took is the level without it with what the part gave. So the phase boundary is exact: a formation is supported exactly when, on every condition it requires, its need with what the part took is at most the level without the part with what the part gave, that is, when the part's cumulative net draw stays within the margin by which the levels without it meet the need. A part closes something that could form only through a net draw, beyond the margin, on a condition it requires (finding that condition uses classical logic), and opens one only through a net give. A part giving back at every step at least what it takes closes nothing; one giving no more opens nothing; one giving exactly what it takes changes nothing.
- **Four cases.** Compared by inclusion, with the formations admitted at each step, a part does one of four things (the split uses classical logic):
  - it widens, as a giver from beyond does;
  - it narrows, as the clearer does;
  - it keeps the same;
  - it is mixed, each side admitting something the other does not, as a conveyor is.

  Mixed parts are not ordered by inclusion. Ordering them needs a measure, which is a further premise, and inclusion licenses no imposed ending.
- **Levels.** What holds within a region holds within every region inside it. What is lost beyond a region is lost beyond every region inside it: the smaller the region, the more lies beyond it. Nothing lies beyond the whole. The clearer is active and supported at every step and closes nothing within itself and the source it depends on; beyond them, the dweller is admitted without the clearer's part and not with it. So a part can continue while closing what lies beyond its dependence. "Only non-degenerating dynamics continue indefinitely" fails for parts, while the whole has nowhere to push the loss.
- **Represented boundaries.** A formation renews over a region of conditions when it is active and its step keeps every dependence on every condition of the region it takes part in. Compared with its actual dependence:
  - over a region covering every condition it takes part in, this is renewal at the step;
  - over a region covering every condition it requires, with requirements not tightening, it keeps its own support yet may undermine another: the clearer renews over its resource and undermines at step one;
  - over a region omitting a requirement, its own draw can undermine its own dependence, and it loses its support.

  Ren says a center and its boundary lie within the same cognitive dynamics. Here a region is a parameter of the statement, with no status outside the unfolding.

The existing phase results remain in `Becoming.Continuation`:
- no condition is drawn down without end (`excess_bounded`);
- joint excess bringing a level below a need ends what depends on it at that need (`joint_excess_ends`).

**AI reading, pending Ren:**
- widening, narrowing and keeping the same read as Ren's generative, degenerative and neutral;
- the regions read as self, system and whole, and as the boundary a center represents.

The comparison counts formations admitted, not the renewal capacity or richness of relations Ren also named. These theorems order the criteria by strength and do not say which one a formation should follow.

**供给与返回（第一部分，P40）。** 一个形成的延续在某步被另一个供给，是指另一个在该步向它当时所需的条件给予（`Flow.FedBy`）。以下不用公理，陈述一个延续实际依存于什么，以及损失怎样返回（[Becoming.Interdependence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Interdependence.lean)）；形式化与名称是 AI 的。

- **供给闭包。** 对供给封闭的形成区域含其成员的每个供给者（`Flow.FedClosed`）。一个形成的供给闭包，是含它、对供给封闭的每个区域的公共部分（`Flow.WithinFeeding`）：它含该形成与每个供给者，可传递，本身对供给封闭，所以是含该形成、对供给封闭的最小区域。闭包之外的，在任何一步都不向闭包成员当时所需的条件给予（`Flow.fedClosed_within`、`Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`；[feeding-closure](../results/records/feeding-closure.md)）。`Clearing` 中，§6.8 沿依存的区域恰是各形成的供给闭包（`Clearing.dependenceRegion_iff`）。
- **消耗随其供给者终结。** 在平衡、只有活跃者给予、活动需要支持之下，一个形成在其活跃的每一步都从一个条件取用，而没有被支持者向该条件给予，它至多连续活跃该条件起始水平那么多步（`Flow.Balances.unfed_consumption_ends`）。供给它的都不被支持时就是这样，所以它的消耗随其供给者终结（`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`；[consumption-ends-with-feeders](../results/records/consumption-ends-with-feeders.md)）。
- **终结经供给者返回。** `Overshoot` 中两个形成各自供给对方的条件，所以任一个的供给闭包都是整体。第 0 步，其中一个从两个条件都取用。它在其所需的唯一条件上更新，跨过这一步保住自身支持，而它的汲取侵蚀另一个的依存。另一个从第 1 步起不被支持，第一个此后至多再活跃一步，从第 2 步起没有任何被支持者（`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`）。各自只从自己的条件取用时，两者每步都延续（`Overshoot.steady_continues`）。所以只在所需条件上表示边界还不够：延续所依存的，到达供给者的延续。
- **闭包之外的不必返回。** `Clearing` 中栖居者在清除者的闭包之外。闭包之内的每步都被支持，清除者始终活跃，而栖居者终结（`Clearing.dweller_beyond`、`Clearing.closure_supported`）。

例子中的终结由其活动规定，例子表明它们与平衡、支持和依存相容；终结的推出在一般定理中。以扣留水平作的比较固定交换的其余部分，所以经动力学返回的终结，在实际的流上、对照另一个参照流来表明。

**Feeding and return (Part I, P40).** The continuation of a formation is fed by another at a step when the other gives, at that step, to a condition it then requires (`Flow.FedBy`). The following states, with no axiom, what a continuation actually depends on and how a loss returns (`Becoming.Interdependence`); the formalization and names are the AI's.

- **The feeding closure.** A region of formations closed under feeding holds every feeder of its members (`Flow.FedClosed`). A formation's feeding closure is what lies in every region closed under feeding that holds it (`Flow.WithinFeeding`). It holds the formation and every feeder, is transitive and is itself closed under feeding, so it is the least region closed under feeding that holds the formation. Nothing beyond it gives, at any step, to a condition a member then requires (`Flow.fedClosed_within`, `Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`). In `Clearing` the region along dependence of section 6.8 is each formation's feeding closure (`Clearing.dependenceRegion_iff`).
- **Consumption ends with its feeders.** Under balance, with only active formations giving and activity needing support, a formation that takes from a condition at every step at which it is active, while nothing supported gives to the condition, is active for at most as many consecutive steps as the condition's level at the start (`Flow.Balances.unfed_consumption_ends`). So it is where everything feeding it is unsupported: its consumption ends with its feeders (`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`).
- **An ending returns through a feeder.** In `Overshoot` each of two formations feeds the other's condition, so the feeding closure of either is the whole. At step 0 one takes from both conditions. It renews over the one condition it requires and keeps its own support across the step, while its draw undermines the other's dependence. The other is unsupported from step 1 on, the first is active for at most one step more, and from step 2 on nothing is supported (`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`). Where each takes only from its own condition, both continue at every step (`Overshoot.steady_continues`). So a boundary represented over the conditions a formation requires is not enough: what its continuation depends on reaches the continuation of its feeders.
- **What lies beyond the closure need not return.** In `Clearing` the dweller lies beyond the clearer's closure. Everything within the closure is supported at every step and the clearer is active throughout, while the dweller ends (`Clearing.dweller_beyond`, `Clearing.closure_supported`).

The witnesses' endings are stipulated in their activity and shown consistent with balance, support and dependence; the general theorems derive endings. Comparisons by withheld levels keep the rest of the exchange fixed, so an ending that returns through the dynamics is shown on the actual flow, against a separate reference flow.

**反馈与所及（第一部分，P42）。** 一个形成受另一个影响，是指另一个与它所依存的条件交换（`Flow.AffectedBy`）。以下不用公理，陈述一个形成自身的反馈到达多远、一个部分的交换到达多远，以及有与无一个部分的比较在哪里决定（[Becoming.Feedback](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Feedback.lean)）；形式化与名称是 AI 的。

- **五个层次**（[levels-of-closure](../results/records/levels-of-closure.md)）。一个区域对一个关系封闭，是指成员与之有此关系者都是成员；一个形成在另一个的闭包之内，是指含后者的每个封闭区域都含它（`ClosedUnder`、`WithinClosure`）；这就是桥接中从一个种子沿边的闭包（`withinClosure_iff_closure`）。供给闭包就是对被供给的闭包（`Flow.withinFeeding_iff`）。对受影响的闭包是影响闭包，即能影响该形成的（`Flow.WithinInfluence`）；对影响的闭包是所及闭包，即该形成的交换能逐环影响的（`Flow.Affects`、`Flow.WithinReach`）。两个形成共享一个条件，是指两者都参与它：依存于它，或与它交换（`Flow.Shares`）；对共享的闭包是系统，系统是类（`Flow.WithinSystem`、`Flow.WithinSystem.symm`）。每个形成在其供给闭包之内，供给闭包在其影响闭包之内；影响闭包与所及闭包都在系统之内（`Flow.withinInfluence_of_withinFeeding`、`Flow.withinSystem_of_withinInfluence`、`Flow.withinSystem_of_withinReach`）。每个包含都可以是严格的，影响闭包与所及闭包方向相反。`Clearing` 中，来源在清除者的供给闭包之内、所及闭包之外，栖居者在其影响闭包之外、所及闭包之内（`Clearing.levels`、`Clearing.reach_middle`）；栖居者的所及闭包只含它自己（`Clearing.reach_centred`）。`Trophic` 是营养级联：狼群在森林的供给闭包之外、影响闭包之内，经由鹿影响森林；一个远处的形成在森林的系统之外（`Trophic.mediates`）。
- **一个共同条件使系统成为整体。** 每个形成都参与一个条件时，每个系统都是整体（`Flow.system_whole_of_common`），`Clearing` 就是这样（`Clearing.clearer_system`、`Clearing.system_whole`）。依存于同一条件的形成，即使没有什么与它交换、彼此也不影响，仍共享一个系统（`Static.shares_unaffected`）。每个形成都依存于一个条件并与它交换时，每个所及闭包都是整体（`Flow.reach_whole_of_common`）。在某一步每个形成都需要一个条件并向它给予时，每个供给闭包都是整体，以它为界的意义，即 `Becoming.Interdependence` 不用公理的路线，就是公理（`Flow.feeding_whole_of_common`、`Flow.feeding_unrestricted_of_common`）。
- **水平随其交换。** 两个平衡的流，一个条件起始相同，从它取用与向它给予也相同，就使它保持相同（`Flow.Balances.levels_agree`）。所以一个形成的支持只计入与其所依存者的交换（`Flow.Balances.support_agrees`）。一个部分所做的，恰经其与一个条件的净交换计入该条件（`Flow.Balances.agrees_iff_even`）。部分的成员都不与一个条件交换时，有与无部分的水平在它上相同（`Flow.Balances.agrees_of_quiet`）；在某步不同之处，必有成员在更早的步与它交换（`Flow.Balances.exchanges_before_of_differs`）。
- **反馈所计入的**（[feedback-and-reach](../results/records/feedback-and-reach.md)）。影响闭包之外的，不与闭包成员所依存的条件交换（`Flow.beyond_influence`）。一个部分的成员都在一个形成的影响闭包之外时，闭包之内的每个形成，有此部分时被支持，恰当无此部分的水平接纳它（`Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`）。`Clearing` 中，清除者无论是否清除栖息地，每一步的支持都相同，而栖居者终结（`Clearing.unregistered`）。影响闭包是上界：其余交换固定时，一个形成的支持只计入直接到达它的部分（`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached`）。
- **部分所及。** 一个部分到达一个形成，是指部分的成员与该形成所依存的条件交换（`Flow.Reaches`）；在某步之前到达，是指成员在更早的步与该形成在该步所需的条件交换（`Flow.ReachesBefore`）。部分在某步之前没有到达的，在该步有它与无它时同样被接纳（`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached_before`、`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached`）。在依存之后才来的交换，影响而无效果（`Late.affected_without_effect`）。成员都在一个对影响封闭的区域之内的部分，所到达的都在区域之内（`Flow.reaches_within_closed`）；所及闭包与系统都是这样的区域（`Flow.reaches_withinReach`、`Flow.withinSystem_closed`），一个形成自身的部分，成员都在其所及闭包之内（`Flow.own_within_reach`）。在一个系统之内的只到达系统之内，在系统之外的到达不了系统之内（`Flow.reaches_withinSystem`、`Flow.unreached_within_system`）。
- **比较在部分所及之内决定**（[comparison-within-reach](../results/records/comparison-within-reach.md)）。两个意义，从一个立场在每步对部分此前已到达者一致时，对有与无部分的水平作同样的比较（`Flow.Balances.comparison_local`）。对任何意义，只要它覆盖部分在每步之前已到达的，有与无部分的水平就恰如彼此包含那样彼此保留有意义者，两个方向都是；两者都不增加有意义的延续，恰当两者都不超出对方：这正是公理的比较（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`）。意义可判定时，增加就是超出（`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_exceeds_of_decidable`）。所以以一个对影响封闭的区域为界的意义，对区域之内的每个部分作包含那样的比较（`Flow.Balances.closed_comparison`），所及闭包是含一个中心的最小这样的区域。`Clearing` 中，清除者的部分到达栖居者（`Clearing.reaches_dweller`）；以各所及闭包为界的意义不是公理，但从清除者的立场，部分失去栖居者，与公理之下一样（`Clearing.reach_counts_dweller`）；以各影响闭包为界，部分保留（`Clearing.system_counts_dweller`）。
- **任意两个流。** 起始相同的任意两个平衡的流，其比较在两者的差别于每步之前所到达者之内决定（`Flow.DiffersBefore`、`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`）。其余交换固定时，差别就是一个部分的交换；他者有回应时，差别也含其回应。狼群的部分到达不了森林，而与鹿有回应之流的差别到达它（`Trophic.difference_reaches_forest`）。
- **比较止于何处。** 覆盖是充分的，不是必要的：部分可以到达一个形成的影响闭包之外，而不改变那里的任何接纳（`Margin.reach_without_misjudgment`）。成员有在一个系统之外的部分，可以被以该系统为界的意义误判（`Trophic.mixed_part`）。以扣留水平的比较固定其余交换，所以经他者回应的效果不在其中计入：鹿的啃食照旧时，狼群的部分缩小，关闭鹿（`Trophic.mediated_unregistered`）；对照鹿有回应的流，同一取用是混合的（`Trophic.responsive_mixed`）。部分给出他者所取时，无此部分的水平可以不存在，就没有以扣留水平的比较（`Clearing.no_levels_without_source`）。

反馈与部分的交换方向相反：形成的反馈至多计入其影响闭包，即能影响它的；它自身的部分到达其所及闭包，即它能影响的。形成是否知道其反馈之外的，动力学不决定。以扣留水平的比较固定其余交换，部分是一个判定的形成家族，所以形成的一个动作不与其其余交换分开，要经第二个流来比较，如 `Clearing.unregistered`；表示承诺离散的步与自然数的量。

**Feedback and reach (Part I, P42).** A formation is affected by another when the other exchanges with a condition it depends on (`Flow.AffectedBy`). The following states, with no axiom, how far a formation's own feedback reaches, how far a part's exchange reaches, and where the comparison with and without a part is decided (`Becoming.Feedback`); the formalization and names are the AI's.

- **Five levels.** A region is closed under a relation when whatever a member bears the relation to is a member, and a formation lies within another's closure when every closed region holding the other holds it (`ClosedUnder`, `WithinClosure`); this is the Bridge's seed–edge closure from one seed (`withinClosure_iff_closure`). The feeding closure is the closure under being fed (`Flow.withinFeeding_iff`). The closure under being affected is the influence closure, what can affect the formation (`Flow.WithinInfluence`); the closure under affecting is the reach closure, what the formation's exchange can affect, link by link (`Flow.Affects`, `Flow.WithinReach`). Two formations share a condition when both take part in it, by depending on it or exchanging with it (`Flow.Shares`), and the closure under sharing is the system, a class (`Flow.WithinSystem`, `Flow.WithinSystem.symm`). Each formation lies within its feeding closure, which lies within its influence closure; the influence and reach closures lie within the system (`Flow.withinInfluence_of_withinFeeding`, `Flow.withinSystem_of_withinInfluence`, `Flow.withinSystem_of_withinReach`). Each inclusion can be strict, and the influence and reach closures point in opposite directions. In `Clearing` the source lies within the clearer's feeding closure and beyond its reach closure, and the dweller beyond its influence closure and within its reach closure (`Clearing.levels`, `Clearing.reach_middle`); the dweller's reach closure holds only the dweller (`Clearing.reach_centred`). `Trophic` is a trophic cascade: wolves lie beyond a forest's feeding closure and within its influence closure, affecting it through the deer, and a formation far off lies beyond the forest's system (`Trophic.mediates`).
- **One common condition makes the system the whole.** Where every formation takes part in one condition, every system is the whole (`Flow.system_whole_of_common`), as in `Clearing` (`Clearing.clearer_system`, `Clearing.system_whole`). Formations that depend on one condition share a system even where nothing exchanges with it and neither affects the other (`Static.shares_unaffected`). Where every formation depends on one condition and exchanges with it, every reach closure is the whole (`Flow.reach_whole_of_common`). Where at one step every formation requires one condition and gives to it, every feeding closure is the whole, and significance bounded by it, the route without axiom of `Becoming.Interdependence`, is the axiom (`Flow.feeding_whole_of_common`, `Flow.feeding_unrestricted_of_common`).
- **Levels follow their exchanges.** Two balanced flows that start a condition alike, and take from it and give to it alike, keep it alike (`Flow.Balances.levels_agree`). So a formation's support registers only the exchanges with what it depends on (`Flow.Balances.support_agrees`). What a part does registers on a condition exactly through its net exchange with it (`Flow.Balances.agrees_iff_even`). The levels with and without the part agree where none of its members exchanges with the condition (`Flow.Balances.agrees_of_quiet`); where they differ at a step, some member exchanged with it at an earlier step (`Flow.Balances.exchanges_before_of_differs`).
- **What feedback registers.** Nothing beyond a formation's influence closure exchanges with a condition that a member of the closure depends on (`Flow.beyond_influence`). Where every member of a part lies beyond the closure, each formation within it is supported with the part exactly where the levels without the part admit it (`Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`). In `Clearing` the clearer's support is the same at every step whether or not it clears the habitat, while the dweller ends (`Clearing.unregistered`). The influence closure is an upper bound: with the rest of the exchange fixed, a formation's support registers only the parts that reach it directly (`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached`).
- **What a part reaches.** A part reaches a formation when a member exchanges with a condition the formation depends on (`Flow.Reaches`), and reaches it before a step when a member exchanged, at an earlier step, with a condition the formation requires at that step (`Flow.ReachesBefore`). Whatever a part has not reached before a step is admitted alike with and without it there (`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached_before`, `Flow.Balances.admits_unreached`). An exchange that comes after the dependence affects without effect (`Late.affected_without_effect`). What a part whose members lie within a region closed under affecting reaches lies within the region (`Flow.reaches_within_closed`); the reach closure and the system are such regions (`Flow.reaches_withinReach`, `Flow.withinSystem_closed`), and a formation's own part has its members within its reach closure (`Flow.own_within_reach`). What lies within a system reaches only within it, and what lies beyond reaches nothing within it (`Flow.reaches_withinSystem`, `Flow.unreached_within_system`).
- **The comparison is decided within what the part reaches.** Two significances that agree, from a standpoint, at each step on what the part has reached before it compare the levels with and without the part alike (`Flow.Balances.comparison_local`). For any significance that covers what the part has reached before each step, the levels with and without the part retain what has significance along each other exactly as they include each other, both ways, and neither adds a continuation with significance exactly where neither exceeds the other: the axiom's comparisons (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`). Where significance is decidable, adding is exceeding (`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_exceeds_of_decidable`). So significance bounded by a region closed under affecting compares every part within it as inclusion does (`Flow.Balances.closed_comparison`), and the reach closure is the least such region holding a center. In `Clearing` the clearer's part reaches the dweller (`Clearing.reaches_dweller`). Significance bounded by each reach closure is not the axiom, yet from the clearer's standpoint the part loses the dweller, as under the axiom (`Clearing.reach_counts_dweller`); bounded by each influence closure, the part retains (`Clearing.system_counts_dweller`).
- **Any two flows.** The comparison of any two balanced flows that start alike is decided within what their difference reaches before each step (`Flow.DiffersBefore`, `Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`). With the rest of the exchange fixed the difference is a part's exchange; where others respond, it holds their responses as well. The wolves' part does not reach the forest, while the difference from the flow in which the deer respond reaches it (`Trophic.difference_reaches_forest`).
- **Where the comparison stops.** Covering is enough and not needed: a part can reach beyond a formation's influence closure and change no admission there (`Margin.reach_without_misjudgment`). A part with a member beyond a system can be misjudged by significance bounded by that system (`Trophic.mixed_part`). The comparison by withheld levels holds the rest of the exchange fixed, so effects through others' responses do not register in it: with the deer's browsing held as it is, the wolves' part narrows, closing the deer (`Trophic.mediated_unregistered`); against the flow in which the deer respond, the same taking is mixed (`Trophic.responsive_mixed`). Where a part gives what others take, the levels without it may not exist, and there is no comparison by withheld levels (`Clearing.no_levels_without_source`).

Feedback and a part's exchange point in opposite directions: a formation's feedback registers at most its influence closure, what can affect it, while its own part reaches into its reach closure, what it can affect. Whether a formation knows beyond its feedback, the dynamics do not decide. The comparison by withheld levels keeps the rest of the exchange fixed, and a part is a decided family of formations, so one act of a formation is not separated from its other exchanges and is compared through a second flow, as in `Clearing.unregistered`; the representation commits to discrete steps and natural quantities.

## 3. 认识与探究 / Knowing and inquiry

**所见范围由动力学给出。** 一个探究策略的所见范围，是它实际执行可达的配置。可靠（sound）的策略在其所见范围上确保它的目标（`Scope.secures`，[Becoming.Inquiry](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Inquiry.lean)）。这收窄了一个原本由应用自行给出的参数。

**The scope of an inquiry is given by the dynamics**: the configurations its actual executions reach. A sound policy secures its goal there.

**判断即一步探究。** 不确定下的判断不需要另立概念：在每个所信配置上都可容许的回应，恰是“判断过程”的共同回应——这个过程在被判断的配置上取一个可容许的回应，然后结束（`commonResponse_judge_iff`）。稳健判断、观察下的纠正与安全选择因此都是同一个共同回应；细化观察就是收窄信念，所以它们保持（`commonResponse_antitone`；外围桥接 [CorrectionInquiry](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/generative-capacity/CorrectionInquiry.lean)）。

**A judgment is a one-step inquiry.** A response admissible at every configuration believed possible is exactly a common response of the process that takes one admissible response and closes. Robust judgment, correction under observation and safe choice are therefore one common response, and refining observation narrows belief, so it preserves them.

**知识。** [知识一章](KNOWLEDGE.md)辩护如下候选：命题知识是这样的真实判断——它的形成或维持，通过认知能力与使其正确的实际关系相连，且其正确不是偶然。知道、理解与会做是不同的成就，[哲学说明](PHILOSOPHICAL-ACCOUNT.md)把它们与共同探究和实践智慧合在一起。

**Knowledge.** The defended candidate is a true commitment competently connected to what makes it correct, and non-accidentally so. Knowing that, understanding and knowing how are distinct achievements.

**已检查的探究条件。**

- **有限可错探究的必要信息条件**：承诺覆盖不超过两类错误权重加证据分离（[finite-fallible-inquiry-necessity](../results/records/finite-fallible-inquiry-necessity.md)）。
- **分支测试**：分支测试等价即双模拟（[branching-tests-bisimulation](../results/records/branching-tests-bisimulation.md)）。
- **有限计划**：可行性等价于按观察分支的有限计划（[finite-continuation-plans](../results/records/finite-continuation-plans.md)）。
- **推导**：推导可靠（[derivation-soundness](../results/records/derivation-soundness.md)）；保留绑定的代入恰在每个位置提供原有根据（[binding-preserving-substitution](../results/records/binding-preserving-substitution.md)）。
- **跨尺度的问题**：问题能否翻译与恢复（[finite-granularity-question-recovery](../results/records/finite-granularity-question-recovery.md)）。全部前缀问题仍可能遗漏中间损失（[prefix-observation-loss](../results/records/prefix-observation-loss.md)）。
- **学习**：取得的绑定进入组合使用（[acquired-binding-compositional-use](../results/records/acquired-binding-compositional-use.md)）；学习程序经实际执行重建（[learned-procedure-transmission](../results/records/learned-procedure-transmission.md)）。
- **证据**：被执行的指令传递候选证据（[mediated-evidence-transport](../results/records/mediated-evidence-transport.md)）；可错见证下的完整可诊断性（[fallible-witness-diagnosability](../results/records/fallible-witness-diagnosability.md)）。

**开放。** 完整核心对科学探究的不可或缺性尚未建立（[scientific-inquiry-foundation](../results/records/scientific-inquiry-foundation.md)）。

## 4. 集体动力学 / Collective dynamics

**共同活动先从实际关系说明。** 不预设对立的利益、筹码、谁赢得更多或固定的共同效用（[共同问题提醒](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-08-collective-destructive-attractors.md#common-problem-solving-and-awareness)）。实际的冲突与差异保留。

**Collective activity is explained from actual relations first**, without presupposing opposed interests, bargaining chips, winners or a fixed common utility. Real conflicts and differences remain.

**市场。** 只有当成功的活动更新了后续交换与生活所依赖的条件、且受影响者仍能发现损害并改变安排时，市场选择才保持在生成区域（[市场与生成区域](MARKETS-AND-GENERATIVE-REGIMES.md)）。已检查的界如下：

- 保护证书下的持续运行（[market-protected-continuation](../results/records/market-protected-continuation.md)）；
- 部分纠正的精确到达边界（[market-partial-correction-boundary](../results/records/market-partial-correction-boundary.md)）；
- 有支持的有限转移（[market-supported-finite-transition](../results/records/market-supported-finite-transition.md)）；
- 有限支持救不回的正种子区间（[market-finite-support-obstruction](../results/records/market-finite-support-obstruction.md)）。

**Markets** stay in generative regimes when successful activity renews the conditions of further exchange and life, and affected people can still discover damage and change the arrangement.

**赢的元吸引子。** 当成功者获得的影响削弱了对产生成功的策略的约束时，当下的优势就改变未来的选择（[meta-attractor](../../meta-attractor/README.md)）。脊柱给出以下结构事实：

- **联合消耗没有单一的消耗者**（§2）；
- **共同参与**：消耗者与失去支持者共同参与被汲取的条件（`consumption_relates`）；
- **差别制造属于共同活动**：多重决定下，扣除任何单独一方的部分，损失照样发生，没有哪一方单独造成差别，而关系读法仍把每个汲取者计为消耗；一个形成的渐进耗竭中，整段的结局由尾部决定，没有哪个有限的事件集合造成整段的差别，而断裂事件造成那一次断裂的差别（[joint-difference-making](../results/records/joint-difference-making.md)）；
- **替代不证明更新**：靠消耗取胜的一方可以转向别的条件而持续，失去者不随之恢复；在逐步读法下，它在失去者失去支持之前被记入，此后到它转向为止被扣下（§2）。

因此，在 N_rel 下，靠消耗赢得的一方不能以“无关”为由把失去者放在意义之外（§6.3）；在关系读法下，“我的一份不造成差别”也不把它移出消耗。这些都不推出责任或义务。

**The meta-attractor of winning**: present advantage that weakens restraints on its own strategy changes future selection. Four spine facts bear on it:

- joint consumption has no single consumer;
- the consumer and the formation that loses support participate in the drawn condition;
- under overdetermination no single part makes the difference, while the relational reading still counts each drawer as consuming;
- substitution lets a consumer persist without certifying renewal.

Under N_rel, winning by consumption cannot place the loser outside significance by unrelatedness, and under the relational reading “my share makes no difference” does not remove a drawer from consumption. Neither yields responsibility or duty.

## 5. 评价的形成 / How evaluation forms

**立场、其区域与意义都在展开中形成。** `own` 是已形成关系的区域，不是所有者；`related` 是非零的已形成关系，即条件、依存、传承与合作。

**一个标准获得影响、认出其对象、得到正当根据、被实际遵行，是四个不同的问题。** 形成的标准可以是错的；学习某种倾向，不等于学到了理由。

**Positions, their regions and significance form within the unfolding.** A standard's influence, its recognition of a target, its warrant and its actual enactment are four distinct questions. A formed standard can be wrong.

**已建立的形成路线。**

- **评价接续**：取得的理解形成评价影响，并进入有根据的应用（[评价接续](EVALUATIVE-UPTAKE.md)、[评价根据](EVALUATIVE-GROUNDING.md)）。
- **在条件下获得正当回应**：起初缺失的评价联系，可以成为对另一存在有根据回应的中介；同一联想学习之后也可以压制这一回应，而不推翻理由。
- **评价者路线**：自我之善，加不受限单调性与容差，给出立场索引、单一历史上的 G0 实例。经独立审查，它已收窄（[self-good-reachable-g0](../results/records/self-good-reachable-g0.md)），并在 §6 中统一。

**比较的读法。**

- **关系信息**：共同组合比较需要关系信息，单项可能性不够（[relational-generative-comparison](../results/records/relational-generative-comparison.md)）。
- **关系读法**：按关系读法，C 把消耗性形成排在它不在的情形之下；逐事件的宽读法则不作决定（[relational-reading-comparison](../results/records/relational-reading-comparison.md)）。
- **基本条件优先**：它独立于 C，并排除每个自然数分数（[protection-priority-excludes-uniform-aggregation](../results/records/protection-priority-excludes-uniform-aggregation.md)）。
- **分类未定时的比较**：见[classification-robust-generative-comparison](../results/records/classification-robust-generative-comparison.md)。

**G0 折叠：一个条件，不是公理。** Ren 2026-10-11（[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-fold-g0.md)）：

> if we don't need G0 as axiom, fold it, at least for part i, and commit

G0 说，已实现的活的内容是正面相关的（`G0`，[Becoming.Metaethics](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Metaethics.lean)）；在主张层面，每个活的主张从每个立场都有意义（`Valuation.Significant`）。P44 表明，第二部分的公理解除 G0 的每处用法（§6.12），所以不需要把它当作公理：第一部分把它作为一个条件陈述，不采用它。下列都不用公理，已检查：

- **它恰是什么。** 在连通、有某个活主张的整体上，每个立场的 G0 恰等于种子加两项边界中立 N_rel 与 N_std（`Valuation.significant_iff`，§6.1）；去掉种子、N_rel、N_std 或连通中的任何一项，都留下一致的反模型（§6.2）。每个活的主张都被某个立场持有时，它恰等于处处 S0 加 N_std，不用连通与 N_rel（`Valuation.significant_iff_held`，§6.7）。
- **它与那一条公理。** 在每个依存网的水平路径上，第二部分的公理恰是以每个主张为活、以从每个立场的意义读正面相关的 G0（`unrestricted_iff_g0`、`Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`）：公理把 G0 的辖域扩到任何东西，并把“正面”减弱为“有意义”。
- **动力学不决定它。** 在任何有活事件的依存网上，处处有意义时 G0 成立，处处无意义时不成立（`Web.significance_undetermined`）。

断言 G0 成立属于第二部分：在那里它是那一条公理的定理（`Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`、`g0_of_unrestricted`，§6.8），从意义读出的正面相关是满足它的最小者（`relevance_least`，§6.12）。§6.1–§6.7 的定理以 G0 或其各部分为假设或为等价式的一边，本身不用公理。折叠以从意义读出的正面相关为准（§6.12）；“fold”与“at least”的读法、正面相关的读法都是 AI 的，待 Ren。

**G0 folded: a condition, not an axiom.** Ren, 11 October 2026: “if we don't need G0 as axiom, fold it, at least for part i, and commit”. G0 says that realized living content is positively relevant (`G0`); at the level of claims, every living claim has significance from every standpoint (`Valuation.Significant`). P44 showed that Part II's axiom discharges every use of G0 (section 6.12), so G0 is not needed as an axiom: Part I states it as a condition and does not adopt it. The following use no axiom and are checked.

- **What it is.** On a connected whole with some living claim, G0 for every standpoint is exactly a seed with the boundary neutralities N_rel and N_std (`Valuation.significant_iff`, section 6.1), and dropping the seed, N_rel, N_std or connection each leaves a consistent countermodel (section 6.2). Where every living claim is held by some standpoint, it is exactly S0 at every standpoint with N_std, with neither connection nor N_rel (`Valuation.significant_iff_held`, section 6.7).
- **It and the one axiom.** On the level paths of every web, Part II's axiom is exactly G0 with every claim living and positive relevance read as significance from every standpoint (`unrestricted_iff_g0`, `Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`): the axiom widens G0's scope to anything and weakens “positive” to “has significance”.
- **The dynamics do not decide it.** On any web with an event that lives, G0 holds where everything has significance and fails where nothing has (`Web.significance_undetermined`).

Asserting G0 belongs to Part II, where it is a theorem of the one axiom (`Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`, `g0_of_unrestricted`, section 6.8), and positive relevance read from significance is the least satisfying it (`relevance_least`, section 6.12). The theorems of sections 6.1–6.7 take G0 or its parts as hypotheses or as one side of an equivalence, and use no axiom themselves. The fold rests on positive relevance read from significance (section 6.12); the readings of “fold”, of “at least” and of positive relevance are the AI's, pending Ren.

## 第一部分的总陈述 / Part I stated whole

本节收束第一部分（AI 的组织，按 Ren“let's aim for the strongest claim without axiom, as the first part”）。它不用公理，伦理的与实质的都不用；只用表示的承诺：离散的步、自然数的量、共同索引，以及被计数的规定可判定。在此之下，它说三件事。其中每条定理都已在 Lean 中检查，核心的每条源声明公理报告为空（§9）；定理之间的说明，例如这些规律不排除表示所排除之外的展开、前提是否成立是经验问题，不是 Lean 陈述。

**一、对每个展开成立的。** 以下不加关于展开的前提：
- 生起与止息、依存的形成与消解、开始与不再作为条件而在，是同一种变化，分别读于活动、依存与水平（`change_readings`）。
- 任一段步上，一个量在终点加其间的下降，等于在起点加其间的上升（`over_stretch`）。
- 有限家族中，开始成立与不再成立同步（`keep_pace`）。
- 凡每步的成立者被列出，其计数就是计数表：平衡，加上跨一步不再成立的不多于步前所列的。每个计数表都由某个列出实现，所以计数没有计数表之外的规律（`tally_iff`、`tally_iff_counted`、`counting_laws`）。

这些规律不排除表示所排除之外的任何展开。所以它们定位关于整体的陈述，不是动力学内容。

**对每个变化之律成立的。** 以下也不加前提；“某个列表持有这些构型”不对任何展开假设，写进用到它的规律（`Becoming.Recurrence`）：
- 有限多构型中的序列从每一步起都重访，任何过程的运行也是；从不重访需要开放（`revisits_within`、`Run.revisits_from`、`unlisted_of_new`）。
- 有标记的变化在有限多构型中不复现（`Marks.change_stops`）。
- 确定的律下，失去的可能保持失去，被合并的保持合并（`Law.lost_stays`、`Law.merged_stays`）。
- 有限多构型中，律不合并任何东西，恰是每个构型都回返，恰是每个构型在每一步都可得；不合并时没有量上升，上升需要合并（`Law.lossless_iff_returns`、`Law.returns_iff_keeps`、`Law.rise_not_lossless`）。开放的世界把这三者分开（`Law.succ_open`、`Law.pred_open`）。
- 保留其历史，每个律都不合并、不回返、开端不能再有、需要开放；有限多构型中的当下遗忘（`Law.withHistory_unlisted`、`Law.withHistory_loses_beginning`、`Law.present_forgets`）。

**二、每个前提加了什么。** 前提是条件定理的假设，不是规律。八个前提彼此独立，每个都划出一个真部分（`World.contingent`、`World.independent`）。
- **计数。** 在八个前提的 256 个选择之下，计数的规律只有两种：计数表；或者计数表，加上没有关系项活跃的一步上没有生起。后者恰在选择包含平衡与体现，并包含只有活跃者给予、或同时包含只有活跃者取用与耗散时成立（`World.counting_under`、`World.counting_binds_iff`）。从某步起被围住时，恰在选中平衡、体现与耗散时还多一条：从某步起的变化界，与此后没有关系项活跃的一步上没有生起（`World.enclosed_counting_under`、`World.enclosed_change_bound_iff`）。有限世界的每一步都被围住：在那里，选中平衡、体现与耗散就使没有关系项活跃的一步上没有生起，并给出从第 0 步起的变化界，计数也总有界；所以 256 个选择在有限世界中给出三种规律（`World.finite_counting_under`、`World.finite_counting_binds_iff`、`World.finite_change_bound_iff`）。有限世界与从某步起被围住的世界恰在选择约束变化且约束计数时有相同的计数规律，全部八个前提是其情形（`World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting_under`、`World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting`）。每个前提都起约束，即每个前提的条件都有实例时，八个前提之下计数的规律只多出有某个生起（`World.binding_counting`）；每个选择之下所选前提都起约束时，至多多出三条：有关系项活跃、有某个生起、在有关系项活跃的一步上有生起，每条恰在何种选择之下多出都已刻画（`World.binding_counting_under`、`World.binding_activity_iff`、`World.binding_arising_iff`、`World.binding_joint_iff`）。没有有限世界使每个前提都起约束（`World.Finite.not_binds`），有限世界中除普查外每个前提都起约束时只多出有某个生起（`World.finite_binding_counting`）；有限世界中每个不含普查的选择之下所选前提都起约束时，至多多出同样三条，前两条恰在同样的选择之下，第三条还在选中体现、平衡与耗散时，或选中体现、只有活跃者取用、耗散与有界传递时多出（`World.finite_binding_counting_under`、`World.finite_binding_joint_iff`）。从某步起每步被围住时，每个选择之下所选前提都起约束，被围住与起约束各自加上自己的规律，三条各自恰在与不被围住时相同的选择之下多出（`World.enclosed_binding_counting_under`），全部八个之下只多出有某个生起（`World.enclosed_binding_counting`）。读于所立者，不被围住时规律相同，从某步起被围住时与有限世界中只在平衡与耗散起约束之处不需要体现：被围住时平衡与耗散单独为所立者定下变化界，体现把它带到活跃者（`World.standing_counting_under`、`World.enclosed_standing_counting_under`、`World.finite_standing_counting_under`）；读于依存，前提不加任何规律（`World.dependence_counting`）。所选前提都起约束时所立者与依存的计数规律没有陈述。起约束只是条件有实例：在只闭合一次的队列中，活动需要支持与有界传递对任何活动、任何流都成立（`OnceClosedQueue.dependent_any_activity`、`OnceClosedQueue.transmits_any_flow`）。
- **静止与开放。** 所立者与活跃者都有普查、生起者体现、平衡且耗散的世界，从某步起每步被围住所立者，就归于静止（静止定理 `World.comes_to_rest`）。每个关系项都体现时，有普查、平衡且耗散的世界从某步起每步被围住就归于静止，所以生起不息需要开放（`World.rests_enclosed`、`World.arising_opens`）。这些静止的规律依赖自然数的单位：以它计，耗散每次交换至少损失一个单位。让单位在每步细分而不算入细分，静止定理的其余前提都满足而生起不息（`Emission`、`Alternation`）。细分本身是一种变化：把它算作只给不取的形成，在细分的单位下平衡就是脊柱的平衡，而脊柱的耗散排除只给不取；所以算入细分，脊柱的前提不添任何前提就带来每一分辨率下的静止，生起不息需要开放（`World.rests_refinement_counted`、`World.arising_opens_refinement_counted`），上面的见证都由细分供给（`refinement_feeds_witnesses`，[rest-with-refinement-counted](../results/records/rest-with-refinement-counted.md)）。不算入细分时的规律作为研究笔记保留：每个封闭家族失去从它取走的一个固定比例时，从自某步起封闭的家族被取走的总量至多是其存量的一个倍数，没有更小的倍数成立（量的规律，`Flow.uptake_bound`、`World.taken_bound`、`amount_sharp`）；由它，从这样的家族取用至少 1/p 单位的次数以其存量为界，围住之内增长、下降、生起或止息至少 1/p 单位的次数以所立者的总水平为界（`Flow.exchange_bound`、`World.growth_bound`、`World.fall_bound`），所以这一尺度的生起与止息都不复现（`World.rests_at_resolution`），储备推迟终结要每个活跃的步使储备至少下降 1/p 单位（`Web.CarriesAt.lasts`）；每一尺度的最小损失只给出交换的规律：一整个单位可以由越来越多、越来越小的转移无尽地带过（每个固定尺度的转移不复现），被取走的总量没有界（`growth_needs_proportion`、`throughput_needs_proportion`）；缺少比例、最小尺度或储备的最小下降中的任何一个，变化都可以无尽地继续（[Becoming.Resolution](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Resolution.lean)，[rest-at-every-resolution](../results/records/rest-at-every-resolution.md)）。
- **依存与储备。** 在活动需要支持之下，构成断裂的那一步选定终止、转化或恢复。储备推迟终结，而不逃脱（`Becoming.Dependence`、`Becoming.Reserve`）。
- **部分与区域。** 一个部分对接下来能形成者的贡献，由有它与无它的比较给出；无它时的水平取扣留这一部分、其余交换不变时的水平（`Flow.withhold`、`Flow.WithheldLevels`），这是表示上的选择。在平衡的流中，以这样的水平比较，关闭需要净汲取，打开需要净给予，相界是精确的：形成被支持，恰当部分在它所需的每个条件上的累积净汲取不超出余量（`supported_of_net_give`、`admits_of_net_draw`、`supported_iff_within_margin`）。在平衡、只有活跃者给予、活动需要支持之下，要求不放松时，支持只从被支持者获得（`gains_from_supported`）。区域越小，其外越多（`Web.LosesBeyond.narrow`）；部分可以延续，同时关闭其依存之外的东西（`Clearing.loses_beyond`）。
- **供给与返回。** 一个延续实际依存的，是其供给闭包：含它、对供给封闭的最小区域；闭包之外的不向闭包所需的条件给予（`Flow.WithinFeeding`、`Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`）。在平衡、只有活跃者给予、活动需要支持之下，供给者都不被支持时，消耗至多持续所耗条件的水平那么多步（`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`）：闭包之内的损失可以经供给者返回（`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`），闭包之外的不必返回（`Clearing.closure_supported`）。
- **反馈与所及。** 形成的供给闭包在其影响闭包（能影响它的）之内；影响闭包与所及闭包（它能影响的）都在其系统之内，即对共享条件的闭包；每个包含都可以是严格的（`Clearing.levels`、`Clearing.reach_middle`、`Trophic.mediates`）。在平衡的流中，形成的支持只计入与其所依存者的交换，所以影响闭包之外的不在闭包之内被计入（`Flow.Balances.support_agrees`、`Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`）。对覆盖部分在每步之前已到达者的任何意义，有与无部分的比较就是包含的比较（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`）；起始相同的任意两个流，其比较在两者的差别所到达者之内决定（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`）。
- **其余。** 运行上的端点丧失与回程、能力与可达、同一与整体、演化、认识与集体动力学（§1–§5）的定理，也只以各自公开的前提为假设。

**三、动力学在何处不再决定。** 以下不由上面的规律与定理决定：
- **前提是否成立。** 实际展开是否满足各前提，是经验问题。八个前提既不决定被围住，也不决定开放；开放连同八个前提，也不给生起不息（`World.openness_contingent`、`World.holding_world`）。每个前提都起约束，也既不决定静止，也不决定变化不息（`World.binding_contingent`）。
- **终结之步。** 在有普查、关系项都体现、平衡且耗散的世界中，被围住时变化不复现（`World.enclosure_bounds_change`、`ChangeBound.not_recurring`）。但若对每个满足八个前提、活动可判定的有限世界都有此后不再生起的一步，就推出有限全知原则（`World.lpo_of_finite_ends`）。对每个有限世界要求生起复现恰当止息复现，而不给可判定性，就等于排中律（`World.law_iff_em`）。
- **混合的部分。** 混合的部分不为包含定序；定序需要一个度量，那是另一个前提（`Transfer.mixed`）。
- **中心。** 延续中的形成必须计入的范围，由动力学推出，在形成层面就是其供给闭包（§2）；沿依存，中心不由它推出（`Clearing.center_not_derived`）。形成自身的反馈至多计入其影响闭包，而它自身部分的交换到达其所及闭包（§2）；它是否知道其反馈之外的，动力学不决定。
- **他者的回应。** 以扣留水平的比较固定其余交换，经他者回应的效果不在其中计入（`Trophic.mediated_unregistered`）；他者有回应的比较须给出第二个流（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`）。
- **意义。** 规律与定理不给任何部分以意义，也不说哪个部分应当延续：在任何有活事件的依存网上，处处有意义时 G0 成立，处处无意义时不成立（`Web.significance_undetermined`）。G0 在这里是一个条件，不是公理（Ren 2026-10-11 把它折叠，§5）。不用公理，它的内容被精确刻画：在连通、有活主张的整体上，它恰是种子加两项边界中立（`Valuation.significant_iff`）；每个活的主张都被持有时，恰是处处 S0 加立场中立（`Valuation.significant_iff_held`）；以每个主张为活、以从每个立场的意义读正面时，恰是第二部分的公理（`Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`）。把部分的扩大、缩小与保持不变读作 Ren 的生成的、退化的与中性的，是待 Ren 确认的 AI 读法（§2）。第二部分在这里加一条公理：在它之下 G0 是定理，保留有意义者就是包含，部分失去有意义的延续恰在其净汲取超出余量之处（§6.8）。

这是[计划 §9](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md#9-走向完整的生成之论--toward-a-complete-theory-of-becoming) 所说四项可检查内容意义上形式陈述的完整，不是必然性的证明；第一部分以不用公理所能得到的最强陈述为目标，还没有表明它就是最强的，仍开放的形式问题见 §8。规律只对这样表示的世界成立；每个生成是否都能这样表示，也是所需根据的一部分（§8）。

This section closes Part I. The organization is the AI's, following Ren's “let's aim for the strongest claim without axiom, as the first part”. It uses no axiom, ethical or substantive. It uses only the commitments of the representation: discrete steps, natural-number amounts, a shared index, and decidable determinations wherever they are counted. Under these, it says three things. Each theorem in them is checked in Lean, with every source declaration of the core having an empty axiom report (section 9); the remarks between the theorems, such as that these laws exclude no unfolding beyond what the representation excludes, or that whether the premises hold is empirical, are not Lean statements.

**I. What holds of every unfolding.** With no premise about the unfolding:
- Arising and ceasing, a dependence forming and dissolving, and coming to stand and ceasing to stand are one change, read of activity, dependence and standing (`change_readings`).
- Over any stretch, an amount at the end with the falls between is the amount at the start with the rises between (`over_stretch`).
- In a finite family, coming to be and ceasing to be keep pace (`keep_pace`).
- Where what holds is listed at each step, its counts are a tally: the balance, with no more ceasing across a step than were listed before it. Every tally is counted by some listing, so counting has no law beyond the tally (`tally_iff`, `tally_iff_counted`, `counting_laws`).

These laws exclude no unfolding beyond what the representation excludes. So they locate the claim about the whole and are not dynamical content.

**What holds of every law of change.** Also with no premise; that a list holds the configurations is assumed of no unfolding and is written into each law that uses it (`Becoming.Recurrence`):
- Within finitely many configurations a sequence revisits from every step on, and so does every run of every process; never revisiting needs openness (`revisits_within`, `Run.revisits_from`, `unlisted_of_new`).
- A marked change does not recur within finitely many configurations (`Marks.change_stops`).
- Under a determined law a possibility lost stays lost and configurations merged stay merged (`Law.lost_stays`, `Law.merged_stays`).
- Within finitely many configurations a law merges nothing exactly where every configuration returns, and exactly where every configuration can be had at every step; merging nothing, no quantity rises, so a rise needs a merge (`Law.lossless_iff_returns`, `Law.returns_iff_keeps`, `Law.rise_not_lossless`). Open worlds separate the three (`Law.succ_open`, `Law.pred_open`).
- Keeping its history, every law merges nothing, never returns, cannot have its beginning again and needs openness; within finitely many configurations the present forgets (`Law.withHistory_unlisted`, `Law.withHistory_loses_beginning`, `Law.present_forgets`).

**II. What each premise adds.** Premises are hypotheses of conditional theorems, not laws. The eight premises are independent, and each carves a proper part (`World.contingent`, `World.independent`).
- **Counting.** Under the 256 selections of the eight premises, the laws of counting are of two kinds: the tally alone, or the tally with nothing arising across a step at which nothing is active. The second holds exactly where balance and embodiment are selected, with only active relata giving, or with both only active relata taking and dissipation (`World.counting_under`, `World.counting_binds_iff`). With enclosure from some step on, exactly where balance, embodiment and dissipation are selected one more law holds: a change bound from some step, with nothing arising from then on across a step at which nothing is active (`World.enclosed_counting_under`, `World.enclosed_change_bound_iff`). Every step of a finite world is enclosed: there, selecting balance, embodiment and dissipation leaves nothing arising across a step at which nothing is active and bounds change from the first step, and the count is always bounded; so in finite worlds the 256 selections give three kinds of law (`World.finite_counting_under`, `World.finite_counting_binds_iff`, `World.finite_change_bound_iff`). Finite worlds and worlds enclosed from some step on have the same laws of counting exactly where the selection bounds change and binds counting, all eight premises being a case (`World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting_under`, `World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting`). Where every premise binds, each premise's condition having an instance, the laws of counting under the eight add only that something arises (`World.binding_counting`); under each selection, where its premises bind, at most three more: that something is active, that something arises, and that something arises across a step at which something is active, each characterized by exactly the selections under which it is added (`World.binding_counting_under`, `World.binding_activity_iff`, `World.binding_arising_iff`, `World.binding_joint_iff`). No finite world binds every premise (`World.Finite.not_binds`), and in finite worlds where every premise but the census binds only that something arises is added (`World.finite_binding_counting`); in finite worlds where the premises of a selection without the census bind, at most the same three are added, the first two under the same selections and the third also where embodiment is selected with balance and dissipation, or with only active relata taking, dissipation and bounded transmission (`World.finite_binding_counting_under`, `World.finite_binding_joint_iff`). With every step from some step on enclosed, where the premises of each selection bind, enclosure and binding add their laws each on its own, each of the three under exactly the same selections as where nothing is enclosed (`World.enclosed_binding_counting_under`), only that something arises under all eight (`World.enclosed_binding_counting`). Read of what stands, the laws are the same where nothing is enclosed, and with enclosure from some step on and in finite worlds embodiment is not needed where balance and dissipation bind: under enclosure balance and dissipation alone bound the change of what stands, and embodiment carries the bound to what is active (`World.standing_counting_under`, `World.enclosed_standing_counting_under`, `World.finite_standing_counting_under`); read of dependence, the premises add no law (`World.dependence_counting`). The laws of counting what stands and what depends where the selected premises bind are not stated. Binding is only an instance of each condition: in a queue closed once, activity needing support and bounded transmission hold whatever is active and whatever flows (`OnceClosedQueue.dependent_any_activity`, `OnceClosedQueue.transmits_any_flow`).
- **Rest and openness.** A balanced, dissipative world with a census of what stands and of what is active, whose arising is embodied, comes to rest once every step from some step on is enclosed around what stands (the rest theorem, `World.comes_to_rest`). Where every relatum is embodied, a balanced, dissipative world with a census comes to rest once every step from some step on is enclosed, so arising without end needs openness (`World.rests_enclosed`, `World.arising_opens`). These laws of rest rely on the natural-number unit, in which every exchange loses at least a unit under dissipation. Letting the unit refine at each step without counting the refinement, every other premise of the rest theorem holds while arising never ceases (`Emission`, `Alternation`). Splitting the unit is itself a change: counted as a formation that gives without taking, balance at the refining unit is the spine's balance, and the spine's dissipation excludes giving without taking; so with the refinement counted the spine's premises bring rest at every resolution with no premise added, and arising without end needs openness (`World.rests_refinement_counted`, `World.arising_opens_refinement_counted`), the witnesses above being fed by the refinement (`refinement_feeds_witnesses`). The laws without counting the refinement are kept as research notes: where every closed family loses a fixed proportion of what is taken from it, everything taken from a family closed from some step is in all at most a multiple of its stock, and no smaller multiple holds (the amount law, `Flow.uptake_bound`, `World.taken_bound`, `amount_sharp`); from it, the times something takes at least `1/p` of the unit from such a family are bounded by its stock, and the times something gains, falls, arises or ceases by at least `1/p` of the unit inside an enclosure by the total standing (`Flow.exchange_bound`, `World.growth_bound`, `World.fall_bound`), so neither arising nor ceasing at that size recurs (`World.rests_at_resolution`), and a reserve defers the end where each active step lowers it by at least `1/p` of the unit (`Web.CarriesAt.lasts`); a least loss at each size gives only the exchange law, since a whole unit can pass in ever more, ever smaller transfers without end, taken without bound in amount (`growth_needs_proportion`, `throughput_needs_proportion`); without the proportion, a least size or a least fall of the reserve, change can continue without end (`Becoming.Resolution`).
- **Dependence and reserve.** Where activity needs support, the step that constitutes a break selects ending, transformation or restoration. A reserve defers the end without escaping it (`Becoming.Dependence`, `Becoming.Reserve`).
- **Parts and regions.** A part's contribution to what can form next is the comparison with and without it, the levels without it being those with the part withheld and the rest of the exchange unchanged (`Flow.withhold`, `Flow.WithheldLevels`), a choice of the representation. In a balanced flow, compared at those levels, closing needs a net draw and opening a net give, and the boundary is exact: a formation is supported exactly when the part's cumulative net draw on each condition it requires stays within the margin (`supported_of_net_give`, `admits_of_net_draw`, `supported_iff_within_margin`). Under balance, with only active relata giving and activity needing support, support is gained only from the supported where requirements do not loosen (`gains_from_supported`). The smaller the region, the more lies beyond it (`Web.LosesBeyond.narrow`), and a part can continue while closing what lies beyond its dependence (`Clearing.loses_beyond`).
- **Feeding and return.** What a continuation actually depends on is its feeding closure, the least region holding it that is closed under feeding; nothing beyond the closure gives to what it requires (`Flow.WithinFeeding`, `Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`). Under balance, with only active formations giving and activity needing support, consumption whose feeders are all unsupported lasts at most as many steps as the level of what it consumes (`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`): a loss within the closure can return through a feeder (`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`), and one beyond it need not (`Clearing.closure_supported`).
- **Feedback and reach.** A formation's feeding closure lies within its influence closure, what can affect it; the influence closure and the reach closure, what it can affect, lie within its system, the closure under sharing a condition; each inclusion can be strict (`Clearing.levels`, `Clearing.reach_middle`, `Trophic.mediates`). In a balanced flow a formation's support registers only its exchanges with what it depends on, so nothing beyond its influence closure registers within it (`Flow.Balances.support_agrees`, `Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`). For any significance covering what a part has reached before each step, the comparison with and without the part is the comparison by inclusion (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`); the comparison of any two flows that start alike is decided within what their difference reaches (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`).
- **The rest.** The theorems on endpoint loss and return along runs, capacity and reachability, identity and wholes, evolution, knowing and collective dynamics (sections 1–5) likewise take only their disclosed premises as hypotheses.

**III. Where the dynamics stop deciding.** The laws and theorems above do not decide the following:
- **Whether the premises hold.** Whether actual unfoldings meet the premises is empirical. The eight premises decide neither enclosure nor openness, and openness with the eight does not give arising without end (`World.openness_contingent`, `World.holding_world`). Every premise binding decides neither rest nor change without end (`World.binding_contingent`).
- **End steps.** In a world with a census whose relata are embodied, balanced and dissipative, change does not recur under enclosure (`World.enclosure_bounds_change`, `ChangeBound.not_recurring`). But a step after which nothing arises, for every finite world meeting the eight premises whose activity is decided, would give the limited principle of omniscience (`World.lpo_of_finite_ends`). Asking of every finite world, without decidability, that arising recurs exactly when ceasing does is excluded middle (`World.law_iff_em`).
- **Mixed parts.** Mixed parts are not ordered by inclusion. Ordering them needs a measure, which is a further premise (`Transfer.mixed`).
- **The center.** The extent a formation in continuation must count follows from the dynamics; among formations it is the formation's feeding closure (section 2). Along dependence, its center does not (`Clearing.center_not_derived`). What its own feedback registers stops at its influence closure, while its own part's exchange reaches into its reach closure (section 2); whether it knows beyond its feedback, the dynamics do not decide.
- **Others' responses.** The comparison by withheld levels keeps the rest of the exchange fixed, so effects through others' responses do not register in it (`Trophic.mediated_unregistered`); a comparison in which others respond needs the second flow supplied (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`).
- **Significance.** The laws and theorems give no part significance, and do not say which part should continue: on any web with an event that lives, G0 holds where everything has significance and fails where nothing has (`Web.significance_undetermined`). Here G0 is a condition, not an axiom (Ren folded it on 11 October 2026, section 5). With no axiom its content is characterized exactly: on a connected whole with a living claim it is a seed with both boundary neutralities (`Valuation.significant_iff`); where every living claim is held it is S0 at every standpoint with standpoint neutrality (`Valuation.significant_iff_held`); with every claim living and positive relevance read as significance from every standpoint it is Part II's axiom (`Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`). Reading a part's widening, narrowing and keeping the same as Ren's generative, degenerative and neutral is an AI reading pending Ren (section 2). Part II adds one axiom here: under it G0 is a theorem, retaining what has significance is inclusion, and a part loses a continuation with significance exactly where its net draw exceeds the margin (section 6.8).

This is the completeness of a formal statement in the sense of the four checkable criteria of [plan section 9](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md#9-走向完整的生成之论--toward-a-complete-theory-of-becoming), not a proof of necessity. Part I aims at the strongest claim with no axiom; that it is the strongest has not been shown, and the formal questions still open are in section 8. The laws hold of worlds so represented, and whether every becoming can be so represented is part of the warrant still needed (section 8).

## 第二部分：一条公理 / Part II: One axiom

第二部分在第一部分之上加一条公理。Ren 提议的公理（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-wanting-continuation-one-axiom.md)）：

> if we want to add one axiom, that is for anything, its continuation into the future has significance(doesn't have to be positive, we could argue as long as not harm), or continuation into the future needs to create more significance(being generative)

Ren 选定路线：“let's aim for a first, then try to archive b”（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)）。在路线 (a) 下，G0 是由这条公理与第一部分推出的目标定理。P39 把这条公理陈述在脊柱上，并推出 G0（§6.8，[Becoming.Significance](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Significance.lean)）。公理读作延续之意义的边界中立：从每个立场，任何东西在每一步的延续都有意义。在它之下，G0 与处处 S0、两条中立与种子在逐步、关系层与整段读法上成立，C 在水平路径上成立。保留有意义者就是包含，所以“不损”的界就是部分净汲取的余量，“生成”就是增加有意义者。第一部分已经限定了这条路线所用的推论：“只有不退化的动力学能无限延续”对部分不成立，因为部分可以延续，同时关闭其依存之外的东西（`Clearing.loses_beyond`）；在公理之下，它所关闭的就是有意义者（`Clearing.free_rider_loses`）。G0 现在是这条公理的定理，不再是公理：Ren 2026-10-11 把它折叠（[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-fold-g0.md)）。第一部分不用公理陈述它的内容，并表明动力学不决定它（§5）；§6.1–§6.7 与 §7 的定理以 G0 或其各部分为假设，在这条公理之下这些假设成立（§6.8、§6.12），其余前提（A、C、L、J1–J4）仍公开。路线 (b) 不加公理，P40 尝试它（§6.9，[Becoming.Interdependence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Interdependence.lean)）：中心的延续所依存的是其供给闭包，那里的损失可以经供给者返回，闭包之外的不必返回。路线 (b) 到达自身及其所依存的（P42 更正：原写“自身与所在的系统”，见 §6.9 的更正）；只在整体是一个供给闭包时到达整体，那时它的判断就是公理的判断。公理所加的，是那些损失返回不到任何人的延续的意义。P42 表明，有与无一个部分的比较在部分所及之内决定：中心自身的反馈至多计入其影响闭包，而它自身部分的交换到达其所及闭包；覆盖部分所及的意义就给出公理的比较（§6.10，[Becoming.Feedback](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Feedback.lean)）。P43 把公理分解为自身的预设——边界之内者的延续有意义，按自身的某种划法成立——与一步：预设不随自身的边界怎样划而变（§6.11，[Becoming.Boundaries](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Boundaries.lean)）。路线 (b) 与公理是同一个预设，“不论边界怎样划”分别取窄辖域与宽辖域；不变这一步承载公理在预设之外的全部内容，所以分解定位这一步，不降低公理。P44 逐处审计 G0 的用法（§6.12，[Becoming.Relevance](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Relevance.lean)）：每处用法由公理解除，公理就是对任何东西的 G0；理由前提须读在活的内容上，如此读，普遍理由由公理推出，不用连通；以可能性读，C 像整段读法那样排序过度汲取，而不扣下任何事件。替换的条件已经具备，Ren 据此把 G0 折叠。这些读法是 AI 的，待 Ren。

Part II adds one axiom to Part I. Ren's proposed axiom is quoted above. Ren chose the route: “let's aim for a first, then try to archive b”. Under route (a), G0 is a target theorem, to be derived from this axiom with Part I. P39 states the axiom on the spine and derives G0 (section 6.8, `Becoming.Significance`). The axiom is read as boundary neutrality for the significance of continuation: from every standpoint, the continuation of anything has significance at every step. Under it G0, S0 at every standpoint, both neutralities and a seed hold on the stepwise, relation and whole-run readings, and C holds on level paths. Retaining what has significance is inclusion, so the boundary of not harm is the margin of a part's net draw, and being generative is adding what has significance. Part I already limits the inference this route uses: “only dynamics that don't degenerate can continue indefinitely” fails for parts, since a part can continue while closing what lies beyond its dependence (`Clearing.loses_beyond`); under the axiom, what it closes has significance (`Clearing.free_rider_loses`). G0 is now a theorem of this axiom and no longer an axiom: Ren folded it on 11 October 2026. Part I states its content with no axiom and shows that the dynamics do not decide it (section 5); the theorems of sections 6.1–6.7 and 7 take G0 or its parts as hypotheses, which hold under this axiom (sections 6.8 and 6.12), while the other premises (A, C, L, J1–J4) stay disclosed. Route (b), with no axiom, is tried in P40 (section 6.9, `Becoming.Interdependence`): what a center's continuation depends on is its feeding closure, where a loss can return through a feeder, while a loss beyond it need not return. Route (b) reaches the self and what it depends on (P42 correction: it said “the self and the system one is in”; see the correction in section 6.9); it reaches the whole only where the whole is one feeding closure, and there its verdict is the axiom's. What the axiom adds is significance for continuations whose loss returns to no one. P42 shows that the comparison with and without a part is decided within what the part reaches: a center's own feedback registers at most its influence closure, while its own part's exchange reaches into its reach closure, and significance covering what the part reaches gives the axiom's comparison (section 6.10, `Becoming.Feedback`). P43 decomposes the axiom into the self's presupposition, that the continuation of what lies within its boundary has significance, as the self is drawn somewhere, and one step: that the presupposition does not change with how the self's boundary is drawn (section 6.11, `Becoming.Boundaries`). Route (b) and the axiom are one presupposition with “however the boundary is drawn” at narrow and at wide scope; the step of invariance carries all of the axiom's content beyond the presupposition, so the decomposition locates the step and does not lower the axiom. P44 audits G0's uses (section 6.12, `Becoming.Relevance`): the axiom discharges every use and is G0 for anything; the reason premises must be read on living content, and so read the universal reason follows from the axiom with no connection premise; read on possibilities, C ranks overdraw as the whole-run reading does while withholding no event. The conditions for the replacement were met, and Ren folded G0 accordingly. These readings are the AI's, pending Ren.

## 6. Universal Metaethical Core / 普遍元伦理核心

### 6.1 G0 的内容 / What G0 says

Ren 对伦理质量的回答：

> 我的答案是生生不息 - 一种整体的，不断创造更多生的方向，而这个方向产生的可能性，多样性越高，复杂性/复杂结构的复杂度可以不断的上升，就是一个好的方向

G0 原是 Ren 授权采用的公理措辞；Ren 2026-10-11 把它折叠，它不再是公理（§5）。它的对象从一开始就是生生；“生”的几种范围都可以成立，并可能得到不同的范围。

**刻画定理。** 在有某个活的主张的连通整体上，下面两者等价（`significant_iff`，[Becoming.Metaethics](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Metaethics.lean)）：

- 每个立场都持有的 G0（主张层面）；
- 种子 S0，加两条边界中立 N_rel 与 N_std。

因此，G0 中超出评价者原本已持有之物的全部内容，都精确落在边界中立上。“G0 ⇒ 前提”这一方向几乎是平凡的，内容在反方向与极小性。

**Characterization.** On a connected whole with some living claim, G0 for every standpoint is equivalent to a seed (S0) with relational and standpoint boundary neutrality. Everything G0 adds beyond what an evaluator already holds lies in boundary neutrality. This characterizes G0's content with no axiom. Since Ren folded G0 on 11 October 2026, it is what G0 is in Part I, and in Part II G0 is a theorem of the axiom (section 6.8).

### 6.2 极小性与对手的形状 / Minimality and the shape of rivals

去掉种子、N_rel、N_std、连通或 A 中的任何一项，都留下一个一致的反模型。**每个对手都接受同一条规则，只是限在某个已形成的边界之内**：

- 自己的区域；
- 自己的立场；
- 认同者。

拒绝 G0 的一方因此不必否认规则，只需划出一条边界。争论随之落到这条边界有没有根据上。Sidgwick 的实践理性二元论没有因此消解，只是被定位到边界中立这一前提上。

Each premise has a consistent countermodel when dropped. **Every rival accepts the same rule inside a formed boundary.** The disagreement is located at whether that boundary is warranted.

### 6.3 从展开读出连通与消耗 / Connection and consumption from the unfolding

L3 可以给出 `living` 与 `related` 两项对应（[becoming-renewal-reading](../results/records/becoming-renewal-reading.md)）：

- `living` 取关系读法，即活跃且在该运行中不消耗的形成事件；
- `related` 取共同参与某一条件。

由此得到三点：

- **连通在对应纪律下由共同条件给出。** 每个活的形成都参与的共同条件，使整体连通；于是在那里 G0 恰等于种子加 N_rel 加 N_std（`significant_iff_of_common`）。条件互不相交时，整体可以不连通（`Separate.not_connected`）。但这依赖什么算条件、什么算参与：加入一个人人在水平 0 依存的条件即可免费连通，而人人汲取的过粗的共同条件又会在其依存落空时使 living 变空。一条对应纪律（[condition-correspondence-discipline](../results/records/condition-correspondence-discipline.md)）只在一方汲取、另一方以正水平依存时才经由条件相关：惰性条件不再连通任何东西（`inert_connects`）；每个活的事件都承担于其上、有活的汲取者与活的依存者的条件，仍使整体连通（`connected_of_renewable`）；经由一个条件的连通由它的活的使用造成（`Shared.connection_by_living_use`）。交互是结构上的共现（一方汲取、另一方以正水平依存，在任何步上），还不排定时间，也不问效果；共同依存是纪律所省略的关系，不是没有关系。纪律排除不了人人以正水平依存、被一个活的事件使用一次的被更新条件（`one_use_connects`），那是一个须由应用检验的事实主张。
- **意义沿共同参与传递。** 消耗者与被消耗者共同参与被汲取的条件；在 N_rel 下，一个立场若珍视某个消耗者的活动，就必须珍视被它消耗的形成的每个活的事件（`valuing_consumption_reaches`）。传递不用到消耗本身。
- **直接的关系（候选）。** 以一类关系项读，交互之外还有两种直接的 `related`：以之为生（在某步汲取一个关系项并以正需要依存于它）与世系（参与一个关系项开始作为条件而在之步，并在该步给予它）。以之为生与交互一样不免费：它需要实际的汲取与能落空的依存；在 `Dependent` 之下所依的关系项在该步作为条件而在，体现时在该步活跃，但它不必体现，也不必活跃（`Drowsy`）。两者的定义都不含体现：这里进入估值的是一类读法的同一对应。它们使只依存于对方的蜂与花、各由前一代给予的世代连通，世代那里 G0 恰等于种子加 N_rel 加 N_std；在 N_rel 下，珍视狐的进食就到达兔的活，珍视给予的形成就到达生起者的活的事件。是否采用它们属于 Ren；`living` 与 C 不变（[Becoming.Descent](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Descent.lean)，[living-on-and-descent](../results/records/living-on-and-descent.md)）。
- **读法相对于一次运行。** 同一过程、同一配置的两次运行可以给出不同的 living（`class_belongs_to_the_run`）。不依赖运行的读法，以及它与 `universal_reason` 的接通，开放。
- **对应义务不变。** 生物圈、气候之类的条件是否真被所有活的形成共同参与，是经验对应；关系读法是否即生生，是哲学论证。

**逐步读法与关系层读法。** 以“活跃且不侵蚀”为 `living`、以各自步上共同参与某一条件为 `related`，前两点照样成立：共同条件给出连通（`Web.significant_iff_of_common`）；在断裂之步汲取的事件与被断裂者共同参与该条件，在 N_rel 下，珍视这一事件的立场必珍视被断裂者活的事件（`Web.valuing_breaking_reaches`）。依存不变时整段与逐步读法的 `related` 相同，只在 `living` 上分歧，而且两个方向都有（`Renewable.readings_differ`）。关系层读法以（形成，条件，步）为主张；一个事件的逐步读法恰是它所参与关系的读法的合取，同一事件可以有一个活的关系和一个侵蚀的关系（`Web.living_iff_relations`）。

**读法由 Ren 选择。** 独立审查判定关系读法的哲学根据尚未确立（[审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING-REVIEW.md)）。[研究 §8.5](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md) 按五条判据比较了各读法，并给出两条有范围的形式约束。其一，任何由有限前缀决定、并记入每次整段更新运行的读法，必记入某次消耗运行（`finite_reading_credits_consumption`）；它是否与 Ren 所采纳的问题冲突，取决于对这一问题的判决是否覆盖越过阈值之前的事件，以及可观测量是否带数量（`amount_reading`）。其二，单一部分的整段 but-for 检验不能代替参与（`Overdetermined.no_difference`、`no_finite_segment_makes_difference`）。[第二次审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-P7-REVIEW.md)接受全部数学，修正了侵蚀与 but-for 的定义，更正见研究 §11；[第三次审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-P8-P9-P7B-REVIEW.md)再修正二者，并把排序、替代与交互的说法收窄到所证明的范围，更正见研究 §12；[第四次审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-P9B-P10-P11-REVIEW.md)把侵蚀改为最小形式，并把继续生成与更多的说法收窄，更正见研究 §15。现有读法读出的都是“不损”，不含继续生成；继续生成陈述在整体的继续上（§2“继续生成”），尚未进入 `living` 的读法。哪一种读法或组合是生生的读法，是 Ren 方向内的规范与诠释选择，本章不替 Ren 作出。

The Becoming layer can supply `living` (the relational reading) and `related` (participation in a common condition):

- Under a correspondence discipline for conditions and participation, a common condition makes the whole connected, discharging that premise. One discipline is stated: a condition relates two events only when one draws on it and the other depends on it at a positive level. Idle conditions then connect nothing, a renewable condition with a living drawer and a living dependent still connects, and connection through a condition is made by its living use; interaction is a structural coincidence at any steps, not yet ordered or effect-sensitive, and co-dependence is a relation the discipline omits; a renewed condition on which every formation depends at a positive level, used once, is not excluded, and is a factual claim for application to check.
- Under N_rel, valuing a consumer's activity reaches what it consumes, through co-participation in the drawn condition.
- Read with one kind of relatum, two direct readings of related are stated beside interaction: living on, drawing on a relatum and depending on it at a positive need, and descent, giving to a relatum, while taking part in the step, as it comes to stand. Under `Dependent` the relatum lived on stands at that step; it need be neither embodied nor active (`Drowsy`). Neither definition mentions embodiment: what enters the valuation is the one-kind identification. They connect the bee and the flower that depend only on each other and generations each given by the one before; under N_rel valuing the fox's eating reaches the hare's living, and valuing a formation that gives reaches the living events of what arises. Adopting them is Ren's choice, and neither living nor C changes.
- The readings are relative to a run; a run-independent reading is open.
- Whether actual common conditions are shared by all living formations, and whether the relational reading is 生生, remain correspondence and philosophical questions.
- Read stepwise (active and not undermining) or at the level of relations, the first two still hold. The whole-run and stepwise readings differ in `living` in both directions. None includes further generation. Which reading, or combination, is the reading of 生生 is Ren's choice; the study's comparison supplies the material.

### 6.4 向每个立场的理由 / Reasons addressed to every position

**D 由 A 导出。** 不以认同为条件的定位表述 D，由 A 与 G0 的不带索引的意义导出（`positioned_articulation_of_articulates`）。

**普遍理由。** 一个以新的活的内容为目标的可靠探究，给每个有通达的立场一项支持理由（`universal_reason`）。规范前提是种子、N_rel、N_std 与 A；连通（在 §6.3 的对应纪律下，相对于一次运行）与可靠性是展开与探究的事实（[metaethical-core-characterization](../results/records/metaethical-core-characterization.md)，复用 [positioned-positive-reason](../results/records/positioned-positive-reason.md)、[positive-ground-open-futures](../results/records/positive-ground-open-futures.md)）。

**理由不是更多的东西。** 一项支持理由不是义务，也不是排序，也不预测认识或遵循。

**D follows from A**, and a sound inquiry for new living content gives every position with access a contributory reason. A contributory reason is not a duty or a ranking, and predicts no recognition or compliance.

### 6.5 比较、提升与实践判断 / Comparison, lifting and practical judgment

**C 与 J。** C 在框架的配置上陈述；J1/J2 的“先基本条件、再活的增益”由桥接层的 `BetterAt` 陈述（[practical-judgment-g0-j](../results/records/practical-judgment-g0-j.md)）。

**L 是被整合暴露出来的隐藏前提。** 情境给每个回应一个结果，过程却可以有多个结果；只要对回应作比较，就需要一条提升规则。在保守的 L 下：

- 比较、理由、协调选择的存活、持续改进无存活者，都对每个框架成立；
- 函数式情境恰是其中的确定情形（`articulation_iff_lifted`，[becoming-spine-instances](../results/records/becoming-spine-instances.md)）。

L 是实质承诺：

- 有一好一坏两个结果的回应，在两个方向上都不可比；
- 传递性需要一个可行的中间回应。

**C 的排序取决于它所比较的读法。** 以两次继续为配置、以读法所记入的事件为实现的主张，C 就是这一框架上的比较（[Becoming.Ranking](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Ranking.lean)，[ranking-under-readings](../results/records/ranking-under-readings.md)）：

- 没有某个形成的继续扩展有它的继续，仅当读法扣下该形成的每个事件；
- 整段读法下，C 把没有过度汲取者的继续排得更高，也把共享排在以消耗取胜之上；
- 逐步读法下，过度汲取者有一个使每个所需仍被满足的事件时，这一事件被记入，C 不再决定它的在场与不在场（恶性增殖与以消耗取胜都是实例），但仍把共享排在以消耗取胜之上；没有这样的事件时（例如第一步就越过支持），逐步读法同样把不在场排得更高（`Immediate`）；
- 在二元定律族中，两种读法下 C 都把在阈值之前停止的继续排在过度汲取之上（`restraint_ranks`、`malignant_restraint`）：那里逐步读法失去与不在场的比较，不失去与节制的比较；有第三个形成共用条件时，与节制的比较也可以失去（`ThirdParty`）。

所以若要由 C 得出“没有恶性增殖更好”，C 所比较的读法须扣下损失之前的事件，或者在 C 之外加更强的比较。以可能性读（在公理之下即公理的比较，§6.12，P44），C 把没有它的继续排得更高，而不扣下任何事件。G0 与 C 是否共用一种读法，属于 Ren 对生生读法的选择（§6.3）。
